### 2026-09-28 (later 6) — Minimum age is now gender-aware: men 21, women 18 (register + profile edit)
Closes the 🔴 flagged in the entry below. India's legal minimum marriage ages (Prohibition of Child Marriage Act, 2006) — the rule our Terms §2 and Privacy Policy state, and what TamilMatrimony / Matrimony.com apply ("18 years for women and 21 years for men", checked on their own terms pages). Until now both screens applied a flat 18, so an 18-year-old man could register.
- **One rule, in `constants/data.ts`:** `MIN_MARRIAGE_AGE`, `minAgeForGender`, `latestDobForGender`, `ageOnDate`, `meetsMinAge`, `ageRuleMessage`. Accepts every gender spelling in use — sign-up `'male'/'female'`, the edit page `'Male'/'Female'`, the DB `'M'/'F'`. Unknown gender → 18 (the lower bound). Tested against the real transpiled code (10 cases incl. "21 today" vs "21 tomorrow").
- **Register (`sign-up.tsx`):** DOB and Gender sit side by side, so either can come first. The picker's `maximumDate` is the latest valid DOB for the chosen gender (18 until one is chosen) and its `value` is clamped to it. **Picking Male after a date that made the member 18–20 clears the DOB and says why** (`onGenderPick`), instead of the member finding out at Create Account. The Create Account gate and `validateFormData` both re-check it.
  - ⚠️ The gate calls the module-level `meetsMinAge`, NOT the component's `calculateAge` — `isFormComplete` is computed ~400 lines ABOVE where `const calculateAge` is declared, so calling it there throws (TDZ) on every render.
  - Also fixed: on iOS `tempDate` started at TODAY, so opening the spinner and tapping Confirm without scrolling submitted today's date. It is now seeded with the clamped value the spinner shows.
- **Profile edit (`EditProfileSection.tsx`):** the module-level `MIN_AGE = 18` is gone; the rule follows the member's saved gender (Gender is readonly there). Picker maximum, the pick warning and `validate()` all use it.
- ⚠️ **Backend not changed** (not asked): `update-personal-info` still rejects only under-18, and `createUser` has no gender-aware check. The app screens are what enforce 21 for men; a modified client or a direct API call would not be stopped.

💡 **Existing data (production, checked): zero real men under 21.** The only two accounts below the rule — user **33** (F, PENDING) and user **34** (M, **APPROVED + active, visible in search**) — have `dob` equal to their creation date (2025-06-25), i.e. age 1. That is the fingerprint of the sign-up default `dob = new Date()` being saved on an early account that never picked a date, not a real minor. Left untouched; user 34 showing "age 1" in search is worth a manual fix.

tsc 55. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-28 (later 5) — Policy agreement shown before every payment; app Terms move to the website's single source (cross-repo)
Part of making online payments compliant before Cashfree production keys (website side: `VaibhavVivaahaWeb/CLAUDE.md`, same date — Terms/Privacy/Refund rewritten, new Subscription Policy + Contact Us).
- **New `components/PolicyAgreement.tsx`** — "By continuing, you agree to our **Terms & Conditions**, **Refund & Cancellation Policy** and **Privacy Policy**", each name tappable, opening the WEBSITE page in the in-app browser (`WebBrowser.openBrowserAsync`, falling back to `Linking`) so the member returns to the same screen. One component so both payment surfaces say exactly the same thing — at a dispute, "they were shown the refund terms before paying" is the whole defence.
- **`PremiumTab` `PlanCard`**: rendered under the CTA on purchasable cards only — never on the current plan's ghost button, never in INFO mode (no purchase exists there). New `isGateway` prop makes the trust line read "Secure payment by Cashfree" in GATEWAY mode. The Proceed confirm restates the agreement in plain text at the moment of payment (`CommonPopup` renders plain text — the card above carries the links; deliberately NOT a change to the shared popup, which backs ~30 call sites).
- **`GatewayCheckoutView`** (PaymentScreen subscription + AddOnPaymentScreen boost): agreement directly under the Pay button.
- **`LEGAL_URLS`** in `constants/data.ts`, beside `APP_WEB_ORIGIN`.
- **App Terms (`TermsPage`, the `TERMS_CONTENT` keyValue row)**: the bundled §5 falsely claimed auto-renewal and cancel-in-settings. Replacement generated from the website's `legal.js` into `VaibhavVivaahaBackend/seed-terms-content.sql` — **not yet applied, pending client review**. Fixes every installed app within 6h of being applied, no release.
✅ **APPLIED to production 2026-09-28** (client instruction). Backup: table **`_bak_keyvalue_terms_20260928`** (1 row, the previous value) — restore with `UPDATE keyValue k JOIN _bak_keyvalue_terms_20260928 b ON b.id=k.id SET k.valueColumn=b.valueColumn`. Verified by reading the row back: parses to content **identical** to `legal.js`, 15 sections, no mojibake; and `GET /keyValue/getKeyValueByKey/TERMS_CONTENT` serves it. Installed apps switch within 6h (cache).
🔴 **Correction to what this entry first said:** members were NOT seeing the bundled Terms with the false auto-renewal §5 — a **12-section remote `TERMS_CONTENT` row was already live**, and it was mostly sound (no auto-renew, non-refundable except where law requires, 21 men / 18 women). Its real defects were **four literal placeholders shown to every member** — `«LEGAL ENTITY NAME»`, `«REGISTERED OFFICE ADDRESS»`, `«CITY»`, `«GRIEVANCE OFFICER NAME»` — and a §5 describing manual, team-verified payment with no mention of Cashfree. 💡 Before claiming what a keyValue-driven screen shows, read the row — the bundled fallback only renders when the row is absent.
⚠️ **Two traps hit applying it:** (1) the `mysql` client defaults to **latin1** — reading the row that way failed to decode, and writing the seed that way would have turned every "—" and "’" into mojibake; always pass `--default-character-set=utf8mb4`. (2) `valueColumn` is a MySQL **JSON** column, which re-serialises with spaces after `:` and `,` — so the stored `CHAR_LENGTH` (6,764) differs from the generated string (6,687) with identical content. Compare parsed JSON, never lengths.


✅ **FIXED in the entry above (later 6).** ~~🔴 **Found, NOT fixed — needs a product decision: the app lets 18-year-old men register.**~~ `sign-up.tsx` (lines ~715/753, "You must be at least 18 years old") and `EditProfileSection` (`MIN_AGE = 18`) apply 18 to everyone. The Privacy Policy has long said **women 18+, men 21+**, and the new Terms §2 says the same — those are India's legal minimum marriage ages, so a matrimony platform accepting a 19-year-old man is facilitating a marriage that is not legal for him. The documents are now correct and consistent; the app contradicts both. Fixing it means a gender-aware age rule in sign-up + the edit page + the backend's under-18 check in `updatePersonalInfo`, and deciding what happens to existing under-21 male accounts.

tsc 55. Rides the next APK (the agreement line); the Terms fix is DB-only once approved.

### 2026-09-28 (later 4) — The checkout mutex now holds until the SDK SETTLES the attempt; callback registration is a stack
Client: "make sure duplicate tap not slips." With server-side order reuse gone (entry below), the button guard is the only thing between a duplicated tap and a second payable order — and it had a hole that was not at the button. `startCashfreeCheckout` awaited `createOrder`, called `doPayment` and **returned immediately** (the SDK call is fire-and-forget), so every caller's `finally` released its guard and dropped the overlay at the instant the Cashfree activity was LAUNCHING. On a slow phone that native transition is several hundred ms of tappable plan list; a second Continue → Proceed there started a second checkout.

**`utils/cashfreeCheckout.ts`:** an app-wide in-flight slot, claimed the moment `startCashfreeCheckout` begins (BEFORE the order is created, so two taps racing the network cannot both mint orders) and released only when the attempt is SETTLED: the SDK callback (onVerify/onError — cancel arrives as `action_cancelled`, so backing out settles too), any failure before `doPayment`, or a 45s backstop. `startCashfreeCheckout` now resolves at settle, so `PremiumTab`'s overlay and `GatewayCheckoutView`'s `busy`/`disabled` span the whole sheet with no change to either caller. A call while one is in flight is a duplicate and is ignored SILENTLY — no order, no popup. The guide's Step 1+2 body is now the inner `openCheckout(): boolean`.
- ⚠️ The backstop is only for a sheet that vanished without a callback (should not happen). While the sheet is UP the member cannot tap the app, so an early fire there is harmless — the later callback still navigates; `settleInFlight()` is idempotent and runs BEFORE `goToConfirm`.
- **Registration is a stack, not one screen's lifetime.** `CFPaymentGatewayService` holds ONE global callback; the guide's mount/unmount shape breaks when PremiumTab is mounted twice (the membership page pushes a second one): each mount overwrote the other, either unmount REMOVED it, the survivor had no callback, the result was dropped — and now the slot would have sat until the backstop. `useCashfreeCallback` keeps the same mount/unmount shape, but `applyTopSubscriber()` re-arms the registration for whichever screen remains, and only clears it when none is left. The onVerify/onError dedupe moved to module level (`lastSettledOrderId`) because the closure is re-applied on every stack change.
- 💡 **Tested the REAL code, not a replica:** the two blocks were transpiled with the project's own `typescript` and driven with a stub gateway (backstop shortened to 300ms) — 12 cases: duplicate tap ignored with one order; guard held through the sheet; tap while the sheet is up ignored; callback settles + navigates once; slot free before `goToConfirm`; fail-fast release; backstop release; late callback after backstop still navigates; two mounts / top unmount re-arms / last unmount clears; onVerify+onError same order → one navigate. All pass. tsc 55. Rides the next APK; no backend change beyond the 13:18 jar already built.

### 2026-09-28 (later 3) — Order reuse removed server-side; PremiumTab's double-tap guard is now a real mutex (cross-repo)
Client measured it on production: five deliberate Continue → back → retry attempts, **two** `payment_orders` rows, 28m10s apart. The server's 28-minute dedupe returned the EXISTING order and its ORIGINAL `paymentSessionId` — a session is minted once, at order creation, so a member whose first session could not open the payment page got the same dead session on every retry until the window lapsed, then it "suddenly worked". That was a real share of the "sometimes it works" report. The reuse path also returned before the `order_created` log row, so attempts 2–4 left **no trace in either table**. Client decision: **no reuse — every tap mints a fresh order**, and duplicate-tap protection belongs on the button. Backend side in its CLAUDE.md (same date); **deploy the 13:18 jar** (verified: `"Existing order reused"` absent from the compiled class, `order_created` + `CHANNEL_SDK` present).

App side: `handleUpgradePress`'s guard was `if (startingPayment) return;` — a STATE read inside a popup callback that closed over the value at tap time, so it could let a second checkout through. Now a `startingRef` (declared with the top-level hooks, above the four early returns per §17) is the mutex; the state still drives the blocking overlay. `GatewayCheckoutView` already had `busy` + `disabled`. Residual exposure if a duplicate ever slips both guards is two ACTIVE orders — harmless (an unpaid order expires); a double CHARGE needs the member to complete payment twice. tsc 55.

💡 Clarified for the client: the "60-second window" I proposed and then dropped was a SERVER dedupe horizon, never a limit on time inside the payment page — that is `order_expiry_time`, 30 minutes. ⚠️ Both today's orders show `paymentStatus: null` — no payment was ever attempted — so the `payment_gateway_inactive` account state remains the blocker regardless of this change.

### 2026-09-28 (later 2) — 🔴 Only the FIRST plan card's button worked; and payments are blocked by a Cashfree ACCOUNT state, not our code
Client: from Home → FAB → Upgrade → plan list, "sometimes something went wrong, sometimes the gateway errors, sometimes it opens and closes immediately, then the confirmation page loads" — and separately, after swiping to Gold or Platinum, **"Continue with this plan" did nothing, not even a popup.**

**🔴 The dead button: `initialScrollIndex` was driven by live state.** `selectedPlan` had exactly two references — initialised `upgrading ? 0 : 1`, and passed to the carousel's `initialScrollIndex`. Nothing read it (the dot indicator runs off `scrollX`), while `onMomentumScrollEnd` rewrote it on **every swipe**. `initialScrollIndex` is a MOUNT-ONLY VirtualizedList prop: the list derives its render window and leading spacer from it, so changing it after mount makes the list re-derive that window while the real `contentOffset` is wherever the member scrolled. The cell DRAWN at a coordinate stops being the cell that RECEIVES the touch there, so taps on card 2/3 hit spacer/dead space — no handler, no popup, no error. Index 0 kept working because at offset 0 the two still agree. 💡 The report read as "only Silver is pickable", but with `upgrade=1` the list is Silver·Gold·Platinum — i.e. **only index 0 responded**, which is what pointed at the list rather than at the cards. Fixed with a frozen `initialIndexRef` (lazy one-time init — `visiblePlans` is empty until the catalog resolves, so it cannot be captured at first render), declared above all four early returns per §17. ⚠️ `selectedPlan` is now written and never read; left in place deliberately as the record of the current page, with a comment forbidding it from driving that prop again.

**The payment failures are NOT ours — Cashfree is refusing the account.** Verified from production's own `payment_log`: at 12:13:07 IST, `stage=create_order`, `POST https://sandbox.cashfree.com/pg/orders` → **HTTP 400 `payment_gateway_inactive` — "payment gateway product is not activated for your account"**, which surfaces as the app's generic *"Something went wrong on our side."* Fifty seconds later the identical call succeeded (order `VVM-936-MUKVT9CO-SM352ALV`), the sheet opened and closed, and Cashfree recorded **`paymentStatus: null`** — no payment attempt exists for it. The app then polled status **17 times over 62s** (12:14:03 → 12:15:05), which is the `outcome='attempted'` path, i.e. the "confirming" page. Action is in the Cashfree dashboard (activate the PG product on the sandbox account), not in code.
- ⚠️ **Ruled out, so nobody re-chases them:** the backend and the deployed jar are correct — the failing order's `order_meta` is byte-identical to the five that SUCCEEDED on 27 Sep (933: ₹2,499/₹4,999/₹9,999 + two ₹149 boosts, all PAID via WEBHOOK); **`return_url` is null on the failures AND on all five successes**, so the `channel: 'SDK'` suppression works; `CHANNEL_SDK` is present in the compiled `CashfreeService.class` inside `target/app.jar` (my first theory — that the 17:58 jar predated the 19:56 commit — was wrong, the code was written before it was committed); and `env`/base URL are consistently `sandbox`, so there is no half-switched credential state. Payments worked on 27 Sep and broke within a day, which lines up with the production/KYC questions being asked in that dashboard.
- 💡 `GET /payments/cashfree/admin/log?day=YYYY-MM-DD` and `…/admin/orders/{orderId}/log` are the tools for this — they hold the real Cashfree request/response bodies. `admin/orders` rows do NOT expose `channel`, so a null there means nothing.

**Two defects left open, deliberately, pending the client's call:** (1) PaymentReturn polls 40s and then says "Still confirming — if money was debited…" about an order the server reports as having NO payment attempt; the `notstarted` state exists but is only reached for two specific SDK codes. (2) `useCashfreeCallback` is a single global slot with per-screen lifecycle, and the membership page's "Upgrade my plan" pushes a SECOND PremiumTab — both register, and the top one's unmount cleanup wipes the registration the other still needs, dropping a result. Same family as the `webSocketService` listener overwrite (2026-09-19).

tsc 55. ⚠️ The carousel fix is reasoned from the code, NOT reproduced on a device — verify by swiping to Platinum and tapping Continue.

### 2026-09-28 — Platinum's membership page printed "5 Oct 2053"; lifetime check is now shared
Client: a Platinum member's **Your Membership** card read "Valid Until 5 Oct 2053". Same bug fixed on 2026-09-27 for the profile tab's validity pill — `PremiumTab.tsx`'s already-subscribed state was simply never touched, because that fix was written INLINE in `(tabs)/profile.tsx` and nothing pointed at the other copy.

Platinum's `durationDays` is the **9999 sentinel**, so `activatePlan` writes a real `endDate` ~27 years out; the plan is sold as "until marriage", so that date is meaningless to a member and reads as a bug.

New shared **`isLifetimeValidity(planTitle, endDate)`** + **`LIFETIME_VALIDITY_LABEL`** in `utils/upgradeNavigation.ts` (both screens already imported from it, so no new import graph and no cycle). `PremiumTab`'s Valid Until row now uses it, and `profile.tsx`'s inline copy was REPLACED by it — one definition, so the two cannot disagree again. The 20-year fallback keeps it generic: a future lifetime pack inherits the behaviour without a second hardcoded plan name (§17).

Swept every other renderer: `settingsPage` only compares `endDate` in a boolean, `PaymentReturn` and `GatewayCheckoutView` print no date, and the backend's `validityText` was already lifetime-aware (`days >= 9999` → "until marriage"), so the activation e-mail was never wrong. ⚠️ `(tabs)/profile_backup.tsx` still has the raw date in two places — dead code (nothing imports it, via the dead `components/tabs.tsx` chain), left alone; it needs this fix if that chain is ever revived.

💡 **The lesson is the recurring one in this file: when a fix lands inline in one screen, grep for the other copies before closing it.** Verified: tsc 55 (baseline, zero errors in the three touched files) and the predicate tested across 10 cases including the 20-year boundary (2046 → lifetime, 2045 → real date), a malformed date, and both arguments missing. Rides the next APK; no backend change.

### 2026-09-27 (later 9) — Notification filters: one scrollable row with a › edge hint
Client: the five chips wrapped onto two rows, and then — once they scrolled — nothing said there was more to the right. Measured: the five labels come to ~398pt against ~332pt of usable width on a 360pt phone, so they cannot all fit; "Views & Likes" → **"Activity"** (longest label, least-used filter, same meaning) and tighter chip padding get **4 of 5** visible at rest.

Now one horizontal `ScrollView` plus a right-edge fade with a **›** chevron, shown only while there is something further right (`onScroll` + `onLayout`/`onContentSizeChange`, so it is correct before anyone touches it). ⚠️ The overlay is `pointerEvents="none"` — it sits on top of the last visible chip and would otherwise swallow that chip's tap. ⚠️ The scroller is a sibling of the `SectionList`, never nested inside it, which is where horizontal-in-vertical gesture conflicts come from. tsc 55. Also: the per-row delete icon shared the chevron's grey, so "open" and "delete" read as one control — it is now the app's error red (`#dc2626`) on a pale rose disc, the same red every other destructive action uses.

### 2026-09-27 (later 8) — Chat gate validated for the notification route (and one server-side gap recorded)
Client asked the same question of chat as of the lists. Checked rather than assumed:
- **App: correct.** `chatscreen` early-returns `<PremiumRequiredScreen/>` when the MESSAGE entitlement is absent, and — importantly — an `if (isLoading || contentLoading)` spinner sits **before** that branch, so a paying member whose `subscriptionData` has not resolved yet sees a spinner, not a flash of the upgrade wall. A Free member tapping a MESSAGE notification therefore lands on the wall, never on the thread.
- **Server, send: gated.** `ChatService.sendMessage` checks the `MESSAGE` planFeature and answers `403 PLAN_UPGRADE_REQUIRED`. ⚠️ My live probe on conversation 135 returned `403 REQUEST_PENDING` instead — the not-connected gate fired first, so the plan branch is confirmed by reading the code (lines ~100–130), not by that probe.
- **Chat-list previews: masked server-side** (`LOCKED_PREVIEW = "🔒 New message — upgrade to read"`), as of 2026-09-17.
- ⚠️ **Server, read: NOT gated.** `GET /chat/conversation/{id}` returned **200 with message bodies** for a Free member (member 26, conversation 135). Thread content is protected by the app alone. Same class as the `hidden_fields` note in §17 — fine for app users, readable by anyone inspecting traffic. Belongs with the deferred auth/IDOR work in `AUDIT_REPORT.md`, not a UI fix.

### 2026-09-27 (later 7) — Chat header showed "BALA" from a notification, "BALA Murugan" from the list; plan gates on the notification routes verified
**Name.** `chatscreen` took its header straight from the route param: the chat list passes the full name, a notification row's `userName` is the sender's FIRST name only, so the same thread read differently depending on the door. The screen already fetches `getProfileDetails(otherUserId)` on open for the verified badges; the header is now state seeded from the route and overwritten with `firstName lastName` from that response — one source, correct from every entry point (notification, deep link, profile). No backend change.

**Gates — validated, not assumed** (client asked). The Views / Likes / Shortlisted-you routes from the new notification screen land on `ListUser`, whose upgrade screen is driven by the **server's** answer, not a client plan check. Called all three as a Free member (843): `/view/getProfileViewers` → **403 PLAN_UPGRADE_REQUIRED**, `/userLikes/whoLikedMe` → **403**, `/mailbox/whoShortlistedMe` → **403**. A Free member who taps such a notification gets the upgrade prompt, never the list. 💡 Two of the three rows are not even created for Free receivers (`ViewedProfileService` and `ShortlistedProfileService` check the receiver's plan before saving the notification); `UserLikesService` saves LIKE rows for everyone, so a Free member can receive a like notification and be upsold on tap — reasonable, and the gate holds either way. tsc 55.

### 2026-09-27 (later 6) — Notifications rebuilt: tap goes where the event happened; one header; no Read button; one-line chat previews
Client batch on `NotificationScreen`. Before: the screen drew its own "Notifications" bar AND mounted `NotificationHeader`, which drew a second title with Read/Clear beneath it; "Read" was redundant because `markAllAsReadByReceiverId` already fires the moment the screen opens; tapping a row did nothing; and a chat notification printed the whole message, so a member could read chats without ever opening them.

**Tap routing** (`openTarget`) keys on the backend category, with `senderId` = the other member in the event (0 = system) — RAW numeric id, §17:
| category | goes to |
|---|---|
| `MESSAGE`, `INTEREST_ACCEPTED`/`MATCH` | the chat — `getConversationByUsers(me, sender)` → `chatscreen` (fallback: chat list / Sent requests) |
| `INTEREST` | Requests → Received |
| `MOBILE` / `HOROSCOPE` / `PROFILE_IMAGE` / `BIRTH_TIME` | title "Asking Permission" → Requests → Permissions; a decision → the decider's `ProfileDetail` (the field is visible there now) |
| `PROFILE_VIEW` / `LIKE` / `SHORTLIST` | `ListUser` viewed / whoLikedMe / whoShortlistedMe (plan gate lives there) |
| `CALL_REQUEST` | the requester's `ProfileDetail` |
| `DAILY_MATCH` | Explore tab · `PAYMENT_*` / `IMAGE_REJECTED` → Profile tab |
⚠️ The permission request and its decision share the SAME category (the field type) — only the title differs ("Asking Permission" vs "Permission Accepted/Declined"), so the branch is on the title. ⚠️ `getConversationByUsers` takes **raw** ids and returns the `Conversation` entity (`data.id`).

**Redesign:** single header (title + count + a Clear icon behind `popup.confirm`), five filter chips mapped to category GROUPS (`groupOf()` — Messages / Interests / Permissions / Views & Likes) instead of the old mixed-case keys that never matched the upper-case categories, day sections (Today / Yesterday / This week / Earlier), pull-to-refresh, optimistic delete, an unread dot + tinted category badge on the avatar, and **chat previews capped at one line** (others two). `Notification.type` is the raw upper-case category and carries `senderId`. `components/NotificationHeader.tsx` and `NotificationFilter.tsx` are dead now (left in place). tsc 55. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-27 (later 4) — Boost countdown started at 18h30m instead of 24h: the same zone-less UTC parse
Client: "profile boost clicked and started the timer from 18 hrs." Measured on user 933's boost (started 11:28 IST): the DB holds IST digits, `/boost/status` sends `expiresAt` as `LocalDateTime.toString()` — the same instant as **UTC digits with no zone** — and `profile.tsx`'s countdown did `new Date(expiresAt)`, which reads that as IST. 24h − 5h30m = **18h30m** on the first tick; reproduced exactly in node (old parse 18.5h, new 24.0h). Now `parseServerDate()` (the helper from this morning's notification fix). The **server's** own expiry logic is unaffected — it compares in one zone. ⚠️ The boost then "disappearing" 65 minutes later was NOT this bug: `profile_boosts` row 2 was stamped `EXPIRED` / `expiresAt = now` at 12:33:10 by **`adminRevokeBoost`**, the only code path that writes that pair — someone pressed Revoke in the admin panel. tsc 55. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-27 (later 3) — Platinum shows "Until marriage" on the profile, not a date in 2054
Client: the activation mail and the profile validity pill must read "until marriage" for Platinum. Platinum's `duration_days` is the **9999 sentinel**, so `endDate` is a real date ~27 years out and the pill printed it. `isLifetimePlan` = `planTitle === 'Platinum'` OR an endDate 20+ years away (so a future lifetime pack needs no second hardcoded name — the §17 rule), and the pill prints "Until marriage" instead. The e-mail side is in the backend (same date). tsc 55. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-27 (later 2) — Chat header: a long name + verified badge overlapped the ⋮ menu
Client report. The header's name row was `flexDirection: 'row', flexShrink: 1` with **no width bound of its own**, so Yoga gave it the name's intrinsic width and the row — text plus the non-shrinking `VerifiedBadges` — ran straight over the menu; the `MenuTrigger` wrapper was a bare `width: 20` that could itself be squeezed. Now the row is `flex: 1, minWidth: 0` (the pair that actually lets `numberOfLines={1}` truncate inside a row), `headerTitle` carries `minWidth: 0`, the badge stays `flexShrink: 0`, and the menu sits in its own `flexShrink: 0` slot with a 32×36 hit area instead of the 18px glyph. 💡 `flexShrink: 1` alone never makes a row truncate its text — the row must be bounded (`flex: 1`) and allowed to go below content width (`minWidth: 0`). tsc 55. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-27 (later) — Notifications showed "5h ago" the moment they arrived: zone-less UTC read as local
Client: "Notification section timing wrong, by default shows 5 hrs." Measured on the 8 AM daily-match row (id 1290): DB `createdAt` **08:00:04 IST**, API `timestamp` **`2026-09-27 02:30:04.553`** — the same instant in UTC with **no zone marker** — and `NotificationScreen` did `new Date(item.timestamp)`, which JavaScript reads as LOCAL. On an Indian phone that is 02:30 IST, 5h30m early. Identical to the chat-list bug of 2026-09-19, which was fixed inline and never shared.

New **`utils/serverDate.ts` → `parseServerDate()`**: adds `Z` only when the string carries no zone (socket events DO carry one), normalises the non-standard space separator (Hermes is stricter than V8 about it), and returns `null` for garbage instead of an Invalid Date that renders "NaNh ago". Verified: `02:30:04.553` → **8:00:04 am IST**. ⚠️ The same logic is still hand-copied in `myChatList.tsx:107` and `chatscreen.tsx:458/585/1032` — they work, but the next zone bug should be fixed by pointing them at this helper, not by a fourth copy. tsc 55 (baseline). Rides the next APK.

Client: the APK on disk was still the 22 Sep build, so none of this week's app-side work had ever reached a phone. `./gradlew assembleRelease` after the safe pre-clean (`rm -rf app/.cxx app/build/generated/autolinking app/build/intermediates/cxx` — never `gradlew clean`, §4.1): **BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 19m 38s** on a machine with 0.1 GB free RAM at the start (it recovered to 1.1 GB mid-build; the CMake phase is the slow part). `app-release.apk` 124.6 MB, 22:49.

**Verified in the artefact, not the source** (the §17 rule): signer SHA-256 `9da0e196…c772bc` **matches the upload key**; `versionCode 4` / `1.0.2` (⚠ unchanged — fine for a test APK, **must be 5 for a Play upload**); targetSdk per badging below. Hermes bundle contains this week's JS: the lazy Cashfree loader (`CashfreePgApi` gate + "Payments need the installed app"), `masterDataFetchedAt` (6h masterData TTL), `classifySdkError`/`no_upi_app`. ### 2026-09-26 (later) — Home bottom banner replaced (WebP, 1.8 MB → 76 KB) and the masterData cache finally expires
Client supplied new "Complete Your Profile / Get Better Matches" artwork for the home page's bottom banner.

**The banner is a keyValue row (`HomepageBottomBanner`, id 11), so the swap needed no rebuild.** Old value backed up to `/tmp/banner-row-backup.json`.

**🔴 The old row's tap target was broken.** It was `openUrl: "external"` with `actionUrl: "/premiumTab"` — that hands a relative path to `WebBrowser.openBrowserAsync`, which is not a URL, and `/premiumTab` is not a route either. Tapping the banner did nothing useful. Now `internal` → `/(root)/(tabs)/profile`, matching the artwork's own "Complete Profile Now" call to action. (The image it replaced was still the **Chennai Super Kings placeholder**, same leftover as the promo popup's.)

**1837 KB PNG → 76 KB WebP, a 96% saving**, and converted rather than linked as-is: the slot renders full-width at 150pt with `resizeMode: 'cover'`, so a 2048×768 source was ~5× more pixels than any phone shows. Resized to **1200×450** (3× a ~370×140pt slot) at q82. Measured at true display size (1110×420 on a 3× phone) the WebP is **RMSE 4.89/255** against the original — indistinguishable; 1024w was 62 KB but 6.16, and the full 2048w was 154 KB for 2.92. Uploaded to the same R2 bucket the app already serves from, `sareeImages/home-bottom-banner-complete-profile.webp`, with `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` — safe because the **filename changes when the artwork does**. ⚠️ Never overwrite a banner in place under that header; publish a new key instead.

**🔴 And the reason this nearly did not reach anyone: `masterData` never expired.** Home only called `loadMasterData` when `HomepageBottomBanner` was *absent*, and `MasterDataContext` restores the blob from AsyncStorage forever. A member who already had it kept that copy until logout (which wipes storage) or a reinstall — so every keyValue edit reached new installs and nobody else, which defeats the point of keeping this content in the database. New `MASTER_DATA_TTL_MS` (**6h**) + a `masterDataFetchedAt` stamp, with `isMasterDataStale()` consulted by Home alongside the existing missing-key check.
- ⚠️ The stamp is written **only after a successful fetch** — stamping a failed one would buy a stale cache another 6 hours.
- ⚠️ A **missing** stamp counts as stale, so caches from before this build refresh once.
- ⚠️ A stamp in the **future** also counts as stale: a device with a wrong clock at write time would otherwise never refresh again.
- Verified across all six cases (no stamp / garbage / fresh / 5h59m / 6h01m / future).
- `sign-up.tsx` and `EditProfileSection.tsx` are unchanged on purpose — they guard on the specific keys they consume (§17), which is a presence question, not a freshness one.

💡 The banner change is live for fresh installs now; existing members pick it up within 6 hours **once this APK ships**. Until then only a logout→login refreshes it.
⚠️ **Security, noticed while fetching the bucket details: `aws.access.key.id` and `aws.access.key.secret` are hardcoded in `application.properties`, which is tracked by git** — unlike the Cashfree and datasource secrets, which correctly come from the environment. Anyone with repo access has write access to the R2 bucket. Belongs with the other items in `AUDIT_REPORT.md`.

tsc 55 (baseline, unchanged). Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-27 (later) — 🔴 Star Match never fetched birth time for EITHER person
Client: "birth time not auto fetched on star match, check the bride and groom detail also if came from profile detail." Both halves were broken, so every Jathaga Porutham ran against a hardcoded midnight.
- **`StarMatch.tsx`** read `astro.star` and `astro.moon_sign` for the logged-in member but never `astro.birth_time`, and seeded BOTH sides with the literal `{hour:'12', minute:'00', period:'AM'}`. The stored value (added 2026-09-20) was simply ignored.
- **`ProfileDetail.handleStarMatch`** built its `viewedProfile` param from name/gender/dob/star/rasi/place — **`birth_time` was not in the payload**, so the other member's time could not reach the screen even once the above was fixed.
Both now pass and parse it. New module-level `parseStoredTime()` accepts the shapes the edit page writes and the legacy ones it tolerates — `hh:mm A`, `h:mm a`, dot separators (`09.15 PM`), bare 24h (`22:45` → 10:45 PM, `00:20` → 12:20 AM) — and falls back to 12:00 AM for anything unreadable (`morning 6.30`, `13:30 PM`, `5:99 PM`, empty/null) rather than matching against a wrong time. ⚠️ It returns `hour` zero-padded because the picker's `hours` array is `'01'..'12'`; an unpadded hour would not select in the wheel. Verified against the screen's own `fmt()` so the 24h `tob` actually submitted is right (`10:45 PM` → `22:45`).
✅ **Verified on the simulator end to end** (Trial member 698, birth time `06:30 AM`; partner 699, `09:15 PM`): standalone Star Match prefilled the Groom tab **06:30 AM**, and opening it from 699's ProfileDetail after an accepted interest prefilled Bride **09:15 PM** AND Groom **06:30 AM** in the same session — both sides, both sources.
💡 The member whose profile you open is not always the bride: the screen assigns sides by GENDER (`lg === 'F' ? bride : groom`), so a female viewer's own details land in Bride and the viewed man's in Groom. Check the right tab before calling a prefill broken.
tsc 55. Rides the next APK; no backend change.

### 2026-09-27 — 🔴 Free members could read paid messages live on the chat list; dead photo-expand button
Two client reports, one real gating leak and one silently-dead control.

🔴 **The live chat-list patch bypassed the server's message mask.** `ConversationService` masks each row's preview to `LOCKED_PREVIEW` ("🔒 New message — upgrade to read") for a member without the MESSAGE entitlement — but that only applies to the REST `chatlist` response. `myChatList`'s WebSocket handler patched the row with `data.message`, the RAW text, so **a member sitting on the conversation list read paid content the moment it arrived**; the mask only appeared after switching tabs and back (which refetches). Reproduced on the simulator with a Free account (696) and confirmed fixed: the row now patches to the locked preview, moves to the top and shows the unread badge, without leaving the tab. The handler now mirrors the server's exact rule — mask unless the viewer can read, never mask their own outgoing message — using the same `message === true | 'unlimited' | {limit}` entitlement shape `chatscreen.tsx` uses. ⚠️ `LOCKED_PREVIEW` is duplicated in `myChatList.tsx` and must stay **byte-identical** to the server constant (verified by decoding both), or a live-patched row and a refetched one read differently.

**Photo-expand button: not a gating bug, a dead control.** Client: "silver sent req, free accepted, the expand icon is still disabled." Checked against production — the server returns `profile.profileImage` correctly for members who have one and null for those who do not, and the expand gate (Free viewer / owner's photo privacy) was behaving correctly. The member they viewed simply **had no profile photo** — only 34 of 80 searchable profiles do. But the button was `disabled` and dimmed with no explanation, so tapping it did nothing and read as broken. `disabled` removed and `openImageModal` now answers "No photo yet — <name> hasn't added a profile photo." Verified on device. 💡 **Third instance of this same trap** (locked Call/Match buttons 2026-09-19, horoscope-only Switch 2026-09-26): in this app a dimmed control must still be tappable and say why — never `disabled`.
⚠️ Interest acceptance has nothing to do with photo expand; that gate is viewer-plan + owner-privacy + photo-exists. Worth saying plainly if it is reported again.
💡 Confirmed in passing that the **2026-09-26 server-side SEND_REQUEST quota is deployed and working in production** — a Free account at 3/3 was refused with exactly `403 REQUEST_LIMIT_EXCEEDED {limit: 3, used: 3}`.
tsc 55. Rides the next APK; no backend change.

### 2026-09-26 (later) — Full QA pass: Explore filters, Trial pack lifecycle, Request tabs (simulator + API)
Client asked for an end-to-end test of Explore (every filter, verified against real data), the Trial pack lifecycle, and the Request tabs. Run on the **iOS 26.2 Simulator via Expo Go against PRODUCTION**, driven with `idb`, cross-checked against the API. ⚠️ **No DB access from this machine** (3306 filtered), so "verified against the database" here means verified against the API's own profile data, not SQL: a snapshot of all 80 searchable profiles was built from `getUserByUserId` and every filter's expected result computed from it. A query bug that agreed with the detail endpoint would not be caught.

**Filters — 11 of 11 correct.** photo-only (34), horoscope-only (35), location Chennai (27) / Madurai (6), star Aswini (6), dosham No Dosham (66) / Chevvai (0), degree ENGINEER (2) / UG (1), employedAt PRIVATE (48) / GOVT (15), age bands 18-25 / 25-30 / 31-40 — every one matched the snapshot exactly. The UI count matched the API too (Trial member, dosham=No Dosham → **66** on screen).

🔴 **Duplicate search results.** The baseline returned **81 rows for 80 members** — user 931 twice, once as Silver and once as Free, because `filterUsers` joins `user_subscriptions` and 931 holds **2 ACTIVE rows**. Visible consequences: the header read "81 Matches found", and `SearchResult`'s `key={profile?.id}` gave two cards the same key → a red **"Encountered two children with the same key"** box on the results screen. Fixed app-side (de-dupe by `userId` on parse + `key={id-index}`); verified → "80 Matches found", no error. ⚠️ The root cause is data: `activatePlan` now closes old ACTIVE rows (verified live — assigning a plan set the previous row to EXPIRED), so NEW purchases are clean, but **legacy multi-ACTIVE rows still exist and still duplicate**. Worth a one-off cleanup.

🔴 **The horoscope-only switch was silently dead for non-premium members.** It had `onValueChange` → `lockedFilterUpsell(...)` AND `disabled={!isPremiumUser}` — a disabled Switch never fires `onValueChange`, so the upsell was unreachable and tapping it did nothing at all. Same trap as ProfileDetail's locked Call/Match buttons (2026-09-19). `disabled` removed; verified on device — it now shows "Upgrade to Silver or above to see only profiles with horoscope" and still does not toggle.

⚠️ **46 of 80 profiles display a stale age.** The filter is correct — it uses `TIMESTAMPDIFF(YEAR, dob, CURDATE())` and all three age bands matched exactly — but the CARD renders `users.age`, a varchar that is never recomputed. User 28's card says 25 while their dob gives 34. So a 25-30 search legitimately returns profiles whose cards read 34, and vice versa. Fix is to derive age from dob at render (or drop the column).

✅ **FIXED same day (backend, needs deploy — see backend CLAUDE.md 2026-09-26 later):** the server now checks and spends the SEND_REQUEST quota inside `sendInterestRequest` (403 `REQUEST_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` when exhausted), and `updateUsedCount` is peek-only for that feature so no APK double-charges. App side: `ProfileDetail.sendInterest()` maps the new 403 onto the existing "used all N requests" upsell instead of a raw error; the pre-check flow is otherwise untouched. Post-deploy proof: `VaibhavVivaahaBackend/verify-send-quota.py`.
🔴 **(Original finding) The send-interest quota was client-side only.** `ProfileDetail.handleSendInterest` does `getRequestQuota` → `updateUsedCount` → `sendInterestRequest` as three separate calls; the server's `sendInterestRequest` neither checks nor decrements. Calling it directly left the Trial member at **25/25 after sending an interest**. So the Trial pack's 25-interest cap (and every plan's `SEND_REQUEST` limit) is bypassable by a modified client, and the two non-atomic calls can drift either way on a network blip. Contact reveal is NOT affected — that one is enforced server-side.

**Trial pack lifecycle — all correct** (698 ↔ 699, both Trial): entitlements match the `planFeatures` matrix exactly (25 requests / 5 reveals / unlimited message / no whoShortlistedYou / no familyLogin); profile page shows "Requests Left 25" and "Contact Reveals 5 of 5"; home chip reads **Trial**. Send interest → PENDING; **reveal while PENDING correctly refused 403 `INTEREST_NOT_APPROVED` without spending quota**; accept → APPROVED + conversation with both auto-messages; reveal → mobile + email, 5→4; **re-revealing the same profile does not double-charge**; chat send OK. Unfriend → conversation `DISCONNECTED`, interest rows gone, **messages retained**, thread still listed, send refused **403 `NOT_CONNECTED`**, contact re-masked. Re-send + re-accept → **same conversation reused** (no duplicate thread) and **the earlier reveal is still honoured — not charged again**.

**Expiry → Free: verified.** Users 710 and 714 hold only EXPIRED rows and `getActiveUserSubscriptionByUserId` returns **plan=Free** with Free entitlements (advSearch false, message false, sendRequest 3). ⚠️ **Trial is configured `durationDays=30` and an admin "assign plan" produces exactly 30 days** (verified live: 2026-09-26 → 2026-10-26). But the Trial rows on 698/699 end **2027-12-31** — ~16 months — because they were hand-inserted by SQL (the documented cohort-grant method), which bypasses `activatePlan`'s `today + durationDays`. **Grant Trial through the admin assign-plan action, not a SQL insert**, or the 30-day limit silently does not apply.

**Request tabs — all correct.** Received: Pending/Accepted/Declined filter chips with counts, pending card with accept/decline, and per-context empty states ("No accepted interests yet" / "No declined interests"). Sent By You: green **Accepted** and red **Declined** badges with a remove X. Permissions: Request Received / Request Sent sub-tabs with a correct empty state. **By Profile ID**: a valid member id returns the profile; an invalid one shows "Not Found — No profile found with this ID." **Saved Search**: Free member sees the centred "No saved searches yet" + Start a New Search; a Trial member saved a dosham search, it listed with `dosham: No Dosham`, and **Use** restored the chip and re-ran it ("filters have been applied").

⚠️ Cosmetic, not fixed: the Request tabs print raw income (`500000/yr`) where the rest of the app formats it (`5 LPA`).
💡 **Driving NativeBase `Switch` with idb needs `--duration 0.3`** — a plain `idb ui tap` does not actuate it (neither gated nor ungated), which initially looked like a second bug. Verify with an ungated control before concluding a toggle is broken.
Test residue cleaned: 709 restored to Free, all test interest rows removed. tsc 55 throughout.

### 2026-09-26 — Profile completion: the nudge lands on the right form; guided "Complete your profile" flow (cross-repo)
Client: "37 fields to reach 100% — do I open 37 popups? give a shortcut." (It is **36**, not 37 — `requiredFields` counted programmatically.) There were never per-field popups: the backend returns ONE grouped `nextAction` (9 possible: PROFILE_IMAGE · HOROSCOPE · HEIGHT_WEIGHT/HEIGHT/WEIGHT · WORK_INCOME · ADDRESS · FAMILY_DETAILS · HOBBIES) and four full-page section editors already cover all 36 fields. What was broken: **the Home widget sent every action to the profile-tab ROOT** with a `tabIndex` param that nothing reads (it belonged to the retired `components/tabs.tsx` flow, and matched on `'gallery'`, a title no action has) — the member then hunted for the section themselves.
- **Step 1 — land on the exact form.** `utils/profileSections.ts` maps every completion field to its destination (`FIELD_TARGET`); `resolveCompletionTarget()` prefers the action `key` the server now sends, falls back to title keywords for an older jar, then to the first missing field. Height/Work/Address/Family/star/marital… open **`EditProfileSection` directly, scrolled to and pulsing the missing field** (`focus` param); photo / horoscope / interests open the profile tab, which scrolls to the card and opens the picker / interests sheet after a 450ms beat (Android will not present a picker over a screen still animating in). ⚠️ **The trigger is a one-shot module-level request (`requestProfileFocus` / `consumeProfileFocus` in `utils/profileRefresh.ts`, consumed in a `useFocusEffect`), NOT a route param** — the first cut used `?focus=&focusTs=` and the client hit it on device: the first tap worked, the SECOND "Add Horoscope" from Home did nothing, because re-pushing the already-mounted profile tab did not re-deliver the params to `useLocalSearchParams`. Same lesson as `mailBox?initialTab=`: treat tab-route params as mount-time only. The horoscope picker is only auto-opened when nothing is uploaded — a stale nudge just shows the card.
- ⚠️ **To open the editor from anywhere, its `data` param had to become optional** — it used to require the profile tab to hand over the section's values, which is exactly why Home could never deep-link into it. Without `data` it now fetches `getProfileDetails` and runs the same **`formatProfileSections()`** the profile tab uses (extracted from `profile.tsx` — one formatter, so the two cannot drift); Save is disabled and a spinner shows until the values are in. The editor's `initial` is now STATE (`null` = loading) rather than a memo over `params.data`; the form and the dirty-check baseline are derived from it.
- **Step 2 — the shortcut: `CompleteProfileScreen`** (HEADERLESS). A hub, not a wizard of new forms: 7 ordered steps built from `missingFields` (`buildCompletionSteps`), each showing what it still needs ("Height · Weight · Marital status"), done rows ticked, the next one outlined, one `Continue · <step>` CTA. Every step opens the EXISTING editor/control (section editor with `focus` + `flow="k/N"` → "Step k of N" subtitle; photo/horoscope/interests → profile tab `focus`). On every focus it refetches, and **auto-opens the next step only when the step it just sent the member to became complete** — comparing that step's missing count before/after, deliberately NOT a "profile changed" flag, so backing out of a form unsaved lands on the list, never in another form. Any row is tappable, so everything is skippable. Entry points: Home widget's ring/summary half, the profile tab's completion ring (both tappable now).
- **Backend:** `calculateProfileCompletion` now carries the action **`key`** in the `nextAction` payload (it was the LinkedHashMap key and was dropped by `getValue()`). Old APKs ignore it; the new APK works against an old jar via the title fallback. Needs the jar deploy for key-based routing; everything else is app-side.
⚠️ **Do not scroll to a card with `node.measureLayout(scrollRef.getInnerViewNode(), …)`** — under the new architecture `getInnerViewNode()` returns a NUMERIC handle and `measureLayout` throws "must be called with a ref to a native component" (hit on device, first cut). The profile tab records each card's `onLayout` y plus the `s.content` container's own y and scrolls to their sum; both are direct children (card → `s.content` → ScrollView), so no deeper nesting is involved.
**Photo / horoscope steps scroll to the control, highlight it AND open its picker** (`focusPulse` state → `s.focusPulse` on the avatar / `s.focusPulseCard` on the Horoscope card, plus `latestRef.current.pick()` / `.horo()` at 550ms). Horoscope only auto-opens when nothing is uploaded yet — a stale nudge should not reopen a picker over an existing chart. ⚠️ The delay is load-bearing: Android will not present a picker over a screen still animating in, and the effect is additionally gated on `isLoading` so the profile has loaded first. The handlers are read through `latestRef` because the effect must NOT depend on them (they are rebuilt every render, which would re-fire the picker).
⚠️ **This was removed once and had to be put back (client, 2026-09-27: "add profile photo click went to profile page not opens the image gallery").** The removal blamed the auto-open for a one-minute stall the client reported — but their description was that the picker OPENED, they selected an image, and the UPLOAD then sat for a minute with no loader. The real cause was the missing loader during compression, fixed separately by raising it before `prepareImageForUpload`. 💡 Two fixes shipped for one report is a sign one of them is a guess; the symptom named the upload, not the picker.
✅ **Verified end to end on the iOS Simulator (Expo Go, production API, hidden test account 700 / 9113000001 / PIN 1234)**, driven with `idb`: Home nudge → profile tab scrolled to top + avatar highlighted → tap avatar → picker → upload (86→89%) · nudge → Horoscope card scrolled into view + highlighted, and again after scrolling away and re-tapping from Home (the one-shot store works on repeat) → upload via the box (→92%) · nudge → Education & Career editor SELF-LOADED with every existing value, saved Education in Detail (→94%) · nudge → interests sheet auto-opened · hub: 2 steps left, Continue → Religious editor "Step 1 of 2" with Birth Time highlighted → time picker → save → hub auto-advanced into the interests sheet → saved → hub reads "Profile complete 🎉 · 36 of 36 · Done". Server confirmed 100%. ⚠️ Test residue: account 700 now has a sample photo, a landscape as its "horoscope", and filled fields — it is `isActive='N'`, invisible to members.
🔴 **Found while testing and FIXED: on iOS 26 every shared-header screen showed NO back button and NO title — just a "…" (More) button at the right.** Seen first on the profile tab's My Shortlist (`ListUser`), but it is the SHARED stack header in `screens/_layout.tsx`, so PremiumTab, settings, privacy, MyRequests, FamilyAccess, ListUser, SearchTabs… were all affected on iOS 26 (Android and older iOS were fine). Cause: `CustomBackButton` was styled `width: '100%'`; react-native-screens wraps a custom `headerLeft` in `UIBarButtonItem initWithCustomView`, and iOS 26's navigation bar folds any bar item that does not fit into an overflow "…" menu — a full-width item never fits. Removed the width (natural size + `hitSlop`); verified on the iOS 26.2 simulator: "Back" + "Shortlisted Profiles" render and Back returns to the profile tab. ⚠️ **Never give a header item an unbounded width again; a layout file needs a FULL reload (cold start), not Fast Refresh, to show the change.**
✅ Test residue on account 700 reverted through the app's own endpoints (horoscope deleted, hobbies `[]`, birth time and education-in-detail cleared → back to 89% with the same four fields missing). Only the sample profile photo remains — there is no profile-image removal endpoint; the account is `isActive='N'` so nothing is visible to members.
💡 **Driving the iOS Simulator from this machine:** AppleScript is refused (`osascript is not allowed assistive access` — the IDE has no Accessibility grant) and the Android emulator would not boot with 8 GB of swap in use. What works is **`idb`** (`pip3 install --user fb-idb`) with the **v1.1.8 `idb-companion.universal`** release from GitHub — the Homebrew bottle and every release after 1.5 are arm64-only ("Bad CPU type") on this Intel Mac, and the v1.5.0.b2 "universal" archive is mislabelled arm64. `idb ui describe-all` gives labels + frames in points (402×874 on iPhone 17), `idb ui tap/text/swipe` drive it, `xcrun simctl io … screenshot` captures (no FLAG_SECURE on iOS). Expo Go must be installed with `npx expo start --go` (the dev-client package makes plain `--ios` look for a dev build and exit), and `--ios` must NOT run under `CI=1`. Helper script pattern kept in the session scratchpad (`ui.sh`).
💡 Nothing is nudged for star/moonSign/dosham/birthTime, maritalStatus, motherLanguage or the family counts by the 9 grouped actions — they only surface through `createDefaultNextAction` once the grouped ones are done. The hub shows them from the start, which is the honest picture. tsc 55 unchanged; backend compile clean. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-26 — Expo Go works again: the Cashfree SDK is loaded lazily instead of imported (SDK kept)
Client cannot use the Android emulator (16 GB Mac, **0.6 GB free RAM and 15.7 GB of 16 GB swap committed** — measured, not guessed) and has an iPhone only, so Expo Go is the practical dev loop. It had stopped working on 2026-09-21.

**Exactly ONE package was blocking it.** Audited every dependency shipping native code: 18 of them, and Expo Go already bundles all but five. Of those five, `react-native-keyboard-controller`, `react-native-linear-gradient` and `@react-native-community/masked-view` have **zero imports** (dead deps), and ⚠️ **`react-native-vector-icons` is NOT a blocker** — it has been here since the initial commit while Expo Go worked all year, because `@expo/vector-icons` is a re-package of the same library (`import font from './vendor/react-native-vector-icons/Fonts/Ionicons.ttf'`, same TTFs, same glyph maps) and Expo Go loads those fonts under the same family names. A first pass wrongly proposed a 21-file icon migration; the client pushed back and was right.

**The fix, in one file.** `utils/cashfreeCheckout.ts` no longer imports the SDK at the top. A `getGateway()` helper returns the service only when `NativeModules.CashfreePgApi` exists, then `require()`s it inside try/catch — the same shape the module had before the 2026-09-21 rewrite made imports static. ⚠️ **Never restore a top-level import of `react-native-cashfree-pg-sdk`:** on iOS the package builds a `NativeEventEmitter` as it is imported, which THROWS without the native half, and a throw at import time takes down every screen that touches this module, not just the payment button.
- `useCashfreeCallback` returns early when there is no gateway — nothing to register, nothing to clean up.
- `startCashfreeCheckout` checks **before creating the order**: an order created in Expo Go could never be paid and would sit ACTIVE until the nightly job expired it. It shows "Payments need the installed app" instead.
- New `isNativeCheckoutAvailable()` if a screen ever wants to hide a Pay button.
- **Nothing was removed** — the package stays in `package.json` and in both native projects, and a dev build or release APK runs the native Drop checkout exactly as before. The client asked for this explicitly.

💡 **Metro including the SDK's JS in the bundle is harmless** — `require()` is resolved statically but evaluated only when called, and the guard means it is never called in Expo Go.

⚠️ **What Expo Go cannot test**, so don't sign anything off from it: payments (always refused there), push notifications (Expo Go uses Expo's own Firebase project, not ours), and — on an iPhone — every Android-only behaviour this repo has spent weeks on: `FLAG_SECURE`, the edge-to-edge keyboard measurement, notification icon drawables, the `GestureHandlerRootView` touch-swallowing bug. Android release verification still has to happen on an Android device.

💡 The iOS alternative is real if the native SDK is needed: Xcode 26.2 and CocoaPods 1.16.2 are installed and `expo-dev-client` pods are present, so `cd ios && pod install && npx expo run:ios` gives a Simulator build (far lighter than an Android AVD). ⚠️ `pod install` has NOT run since the SDK was added — Cashfree is absent from `Podfile.lock` and 13 stale Razorpay entries are still in it.

tsc 55 (baseline, unchanged). Both callers — `PremiumTab.tsx` and `GatewayCheckoutView.tsx` — are unchanged and degrade safely.

### 2026-09-22 (later) — Receipt PDF is e-mailed after every paid order (backend-only, see backend CLAUDE.md same date)
No app change. New backend `payment_invoices` + `InvoiceService`: one "Payment Receipt" PDF per PAID order (not a tax invoice — no GSTIN), sent from `fulfil()` so it fires for webhook, verify and nightly paths alike, exactly once. Member download exists at `GET /payments/cashfree/order/{orderId}/receipt.pdf?requesterId=<base64 id>` if a "Download receipt" button is ever wanted on `PaymentReturn`. Support resend: `POST …/admin/orders/{orderId}/receipt/resend`.

### 2026-09-22 — Full payment-gateway test pass on the emulator: 12 scenarios, 4 methods, 8 findings (cross-repo)
Client: test every payment scenario and method on the emulator and verify what reaches the database. Ran against **production + Cashfree SANDBOX** with the 17:35 jar deployed and the 18:06 APK, all on test user 926 (₹149 boost for repeatability, plus one real ₹9,999 Platinum purchase). **12 orders, 7 paid, no real money.** All four sections now render (the deploy fixed it: orders send `upi,dc,cc,nb,app`; before it they sent `upi` only, which was the whole "cards not showing" report).

**Passed, verified in the DB each time** — card SUCCESS/FAILED/PENDING/USER_DROPPED · net banking SUCCESS · wallet SUCCESS · UPI intent SUCCESS/FAILED · UPI ID (collect) SUCCESS · user cancel · network lost mid-payment · Platinum plan purchase. `paymentGroup` correctly records `credit_card`/`net_banking`/`wallet`/`upi`; every paid order is `fulfilledBy=WEBHOOK`; **21 SUCCESS webhooks for 7 payments (Cashfree sends ~3 each) produced exactly 7 credits** — `markPaid`'s atomic claim holds. `boost_credits` 1 → 7, matching the six successful boosts exactly, with nothing credited for any failed or cancelled attempt. The plan purchase also fired the live `plan_changed` path: the app showed "Your plan is now Platinum" without a restart.
**The friendly-error mapping added 2026-09-21 works on real bank text** — the simulator's bank-decline reasons were mapped, not shown: "could not complete their OTP or other 2FA" → "The details or PIN/OTP entered were not accepted"; UPI's "temporary issue from the bank side" → "Your bank or UPI app is having trouble right now"; USER_DROPPED → "The payment was cancelled before it finished". `classifySdkError` also behaved: `action_cancelled` → cancelled (instant "Payment cancelled", no 40s poll), `order_inactive`/`payment_failed` → attempted (server asked, correct answer shown).

🔴 **1. Order reuse hands back a DEAD payment session.** `createOrder` reuses an ACTIVE order for the same user/purpose/amount, gated on `expiresAt`. But **`expiresAt` is written with IST digits while `createdAt` is UTC** — `OffsetDateTime.now(IST)...toLocalDateTime()` on a UTC JVM — so a 30-minute order (`cashfree.order.expiry.minutes=30`) *looks* valid for **6 hours**. Reproduced twice: a 29-minute-old order failed with `order_inactive`, and a 3-hour-old one gave the member "This payment has expired". Fix the timezone, or gate reuse on real age.
🔴 **2. Three time bases in `payment_orders`.** `createdAt` UTC (JVM), `expiresAt` IST digits, `fulfilledAt` **MySQL `CURRENT_TIMESTAMP`** (UTC, set inside `markPaid`), `paymentTime` explicitly converted to IST. A paid order therefore reads as fulfilled 5h30m *before* it was created.
🔴 **3. A successful WALLET payment strands the member on "Webpage not available".** `cashfree.return.url` is the website page, which then deep-links `vaibhavvivaaha://screens/PaymentReturn` — and the SDK's embedded WebView cannot follow a custom scheme (`net::ERR_UNKNOWN_URL_SCHEME`). Money taken, credit granted, but the member sees a broken page, then "Exit payment?", then the checkout page again *for an order already paid* — two double-payment invitations. It does recover to "Payment successful" after backing out twice. Card and net banking are unaffected (the SDK returns internally).
🔴 **4. The status poll never stops on a terminal failure.** `PaymentReturn.check()` sets `'failed'` but has **no `return`**, so it keeps polling for the full 40s; and because a failed payment leaves `orderStatus=ACTIVE`, the server's fast-path calls Cashfree on *every* poll. One failed payment produced **59 `payment_log` rows and 29 live Cashfree calls**; one historical order reached **210 rows / 103 calls**. 12 test orders added ~198 log rows.
🔴 **5. Every purchase leaves the previous subscription ACTIVE.** User 926 now has **4 ACTIVE rows** (Free, Trial, Silver, Platinum). Gating is safe (the repository default method takes the newest ACTIVE row) but analytics are wrong, and when Platinum expires the member silently drops onto the still-ACTIVE Silver they no longer pay for.
⚠️ **6. Signature rejection returns 401, which starts a Cashfree retry storm.** 7 stored webhooks are `signatureValid=N`, all `TIMESTAMP_SKEW`, with skew growing 905s → 2708s → 6312s → 17113s → 38716s: Cashfree retrying the *same* event, each retry carrying the original timestamp and so rejected again. The skew check runs BEFORE the signature check, so a provably genuine retry is treated as a replay. Verify the signature first, then acknowledge an old-but-authentic retry with 200 instead of 401.
⚠️ **7. Wallet needs the phone number retyped** — the pre-filled `+91…` is not accepted until re-entered, and pressing Pay before that does nothing with no message.
⚠️ **8. An offline "Pay" tap gave no feedback in one run** (airplane mode, after an app restart) although `popupError` is wired to `popup.error` on both screens and the same failure *did* show "Network error" earlier in the session. Not reproduced reliably — needs one more look.

💡 **No invoice exists anywhere** — zero files in the backend reference "invoice" and there is no invoice table. The trail is `payment_orders` + `payment_log` (create → created → webhooks → fulfilled) + `payment_webhook_events` (raw payloads, `orderId`, `signatureValid`, `idempotencyKey`). ⚠️ The 31 stored events have 31 distinct idempotency keys, so Cashfree's duplicates are NOT deduped by key — only `markPaid` stops double-crediting.
💡 **"Pay with UPI ID" still renders on sandbox** and paid successfully; it will not be there on production Android (NPCI, blocked by Cashfree since 2026-02-28). Fine — our UPI buttons are Intent.
⚠️ **Test residue on production:** user 926 is now **Platinum** (was Silver) with **7 boost credits**, and holds 4 ACTIVE subscription rows. Revert if that account matters.
⚠️ Emulator notes: a `uiautomator dump` takes ~14s and hangs permanently if a previous one is stuck (`adb shell pkill -f uiautomator` clears it); `adb shell input text` drops or doubles the first character unless it is sent alone first; the Cashfree page scrolls between steps, so locate nodes fresh rather than reusing coordinates.

### 2026-09-21 (later) — "Not connected" popup: label shortened to "View" (shared dialog left alone)
Client, on the unfriend-then-tap-chat popup: **View Profile** looked cramped next to **Close**. `CommonPopup` gives both buttons `flex: 1`, so a 5-character "Close" claims exactly half the dialog and a 12-character primary squeezes into the other half.

A first pass changed the SHARED dialog (secondary sized to its label, primary took the rest). ⚠️ **Reverted** — `CommonPopup` backs **30 `popup.confirm` call sites** (unfriend, delete account, discard changes, remove photo, block, upgrade prompts), and the client did not want a one-screen complaint restyling all of them. The button layout is byte-identical to HEAD again.

Fixed instead by shortening the label: **'View Profile' → 'View'** in `myChatList`'s DISCONNECTED branch. It fits the half-row `flex: 1` gives it, and the popup title and body already say whose profile it is. 💡 When a shared component looks wrong for one caller, check how many callers it has before changing it — the cheaper fix is usually the caller's own content. tsc 55 unchanged. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-21 — Birth Time is a full row on ProfileDetail, in BOTH states
Client: give Birth Time a full row, and keep the space reserved with a "locked by the member" message when it is hidden by privacy. The hidden case already rendered a `RestrictedField` card ("Birth Time Hidden / Tap to request access"), but it was boxed at `flexBasis: '45%'` — half a row — so the two lines of that card wrapped awkwardly in a narrow tile.

`DetailField` gained a **`fullWidth`** prop (`flexBasis`/`minWidth` → `'100%'` instead of `'45%'`), and Birth Time now takes a full row **in both branches**: shown, and hidden-with-the-request-card. 💡 Matching the footprint across both states is the point — otherwise the whole Religious Details grid reflows the moment the owner unhides the field or a pending request is approved. Every other field keeps the two-per-row default; the prop is opt-in.

No logic touched: the `hiddenFields.includes('birthTime') && !approvedFields.includes('BIRTH_TIME')` gate, the reveal precedence (`revealedBirthTime || religious.BirthTime` — one reveal covers contact + horoscope + birth time) and the request flow are unchanged. tsc 55 (this session's baseline; ProfileDetail keeps its one pre-existing `atob(string|null)` TS2769 and gained none). Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-21 — Cashfree SDK error codes classified; no raw backend/bank text on any payment screen
Client: "have we handled the SDK error codes? none of the backend error display on ui."
- **`useCashfreeCallback.onError` only knew "cancel"** — every other SDK error (`SESSION_OBJECT_MISSING`, `NO_UPI_APP_AVAILABLE`, any `*_MISSING` / `INVALID_*` / `WRONG_CALLING_CONTEXT`) sent the member to `PaymentReturn` to poll 40s and then read "Still confirming — if money was debited…" about a payment that never began. New `classifySdkError()` in `utils/cashfreeCheckout.ts` → `cancelled` / `no_upi_app` / `not_started` (the documented integration codes, `SDK_NOT_STARTED_CODES`) / `attempted` (anything else — a real declined/failed payment, server is asked as usual). `no_upi_app` and `not_started` travel as a `sdk_error` route param; `PaymentReturn` does ONE server check and, if the server confirms no attempt, shows the new **`notstarted`** state ("No UPI app found — pay by card, net banking or wallet" / "Payment could not start"). If the server DOES show an attempt, the normal paid/failed/pending logic wins — the SDK is never trusted over the server.
- **Raw text removed from the UI** (all still logged to console): `createCashfreeOrder`'s unknown `body.message` and `e.response.data.message`; `PaymentReturn`'s status-refused `body.message`; and **`order.failureReason`**, which is Cashfree's `error_details.error_description` relayed from the bank ("Transaction failed at the remitter bank U30…"). New `friendlyFailure(order)` uses that text only to PICK one of six sentences (cancelled / balance-or-limit / PIN-OTP-details / timed out / bank declined / bank-or-UPI trouble), never renders it. `AddOnPaymentScreen`'s proof-upload errors genericised too.
- Net banking, cards and wallets need nothing extra: they return through the same three paths as UPI (SDK callback → `PaymentReturn` status poll, webhook, nightly reconcile).
tsc 55. JS-only — rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-21 (night) — UPI COLLECT ("Pay with UPI ID") is deprecated by NPCI; our UPI INTENT flow is the recommended one
Client asked, from Cashfree's UPI docs, whether our implementation is about to be deprecated. **No.** What is deprecated is UPI **Collect** — the "Pay with UPI ID" row — for merchant (P2M) payments: *"UPI Collect for P2M transactions must be discontinued"* (NPCI), and *"Cashfree has blocked UPI Collect on Android and desktop from February 28, 2026"*. Allowed flows: **Android → UPI Intent only; desktop → UPI QR only; iOS → Intent + Collect, temporarily.** Exemptions (IPO/secondary-market MCCs, mandates/Autopay, eRupi, PACB) do not apply to us.
- **Our app's UPI buttons ARE UPI Intent** (SDK Drop page → hands off to GPay/PhonePe/…), i.e. the flow merchants are told to migrate TO. Nothing to change.
- ⚠️ **"Pay with UPI ID" still renders on SANDBOX and will NOT be there on production Android** — Cashfree removes Collect from its own (non-seamless) pages: "No merchant action needed". We are non-seamless (Cashfree draws the page), so the header-passing duty that applies to *seamless* merchants does not apply. **Never promise members a UPI-ID option in copy** — checked: no gateway-flow text does (the "UPI ID" strings in `PaymentScreen`/`AddOnPaymentScreen` are the old manual-QR mode's MERCHANT id, a different thing).
- A member with no UPI app therefore cannot fall back to typing a UPI ID on Android — which is a concrete reason the card / net-banking / wallet sections added the same evening matter.
- **Website `/pay` page** uses Cashfree's hosted checkout, which shows QR on desktop and drops Collect automatically — also nothing to do.
- **UPI Autopay interoperability** (third link) concerns recurring MANDATES moving between gateways; "does not affect one-time UPI payments". We only take one-time payments — not applicable unless auto-renewing subscriptions are ever built.
Comment-only code change; no rebuild needed for it.

### 2026-09-21 (evening) — Checkout widened: UPI + cards + net banking + wallets; PhonePe-on-sandbox explained; NFC restored (cross-repo)
Client confirmed on their phone that the 17:18 APK lists UPI apps again (GPay, WhatsApp, BHIM + UPI ID), then asked for cards, net banking and wallets, WITHOUT pay-later / EMI / bank transfer.
- **Server (decides):** `cashfree.payment.methods` default `upi` → **`upi,dc,cc,nb,app`** (`app` = wallets). Excluded on purpose: `paylater, cardlessemi, dcemi, ccemi, banktransfer, paypal`, and `ccc`/`ppc` (corporate + prepaid cards, the highest-fee card types — the client asked for the cheaper cards). Still one env var (`CASHFREE_PAYMENT_METHODS`) + restart to change. Jar rebuilt clean 17:35. **Needs deploy — until then orders still say `upi` and the page stays UPI-only whatever the app asks for.**
- **App:** `CFPaymentComponentBuilder().add(UPI).add(CARD).add(NB).add(WALLET)` — never `EMI`, `PAY_LATER`, `PAYPAL`. The ORDER is what really decides; a section the order does not allow simply does not render. Swiper popup copy updated.
- ⚠️ **NFC permission restored** (the `tools:node="remove"` override is gone): it was stripped while checkout was UPI-only, but the SDK's card screen turns on NFC reader mode for tap-to-read and that throws `SecurityException` without it. Normal permission, `uses-feature required=false`. Do not strip it again while cards are enabled.
- 💡 **PhonePe missing on sandbox is by design, and this one really IS the environment:** `PhonePeUtil.getPhonePePackage(env)` returns `com.phonepe.app.preprod` (PhonePe's pre-production test app) for SANDBOX and the real `com.phonepe.app` only for PRODUCTION. Real PhonePe will list once production keys are in. (Contrast with the generic app list, which is environment-independent.)
- **Outcome handling is method-agnostic, checked in code:** `CashfreeService` has no branch on the payment method — success / failed / user-dropped key on `payment_status`, the poll fast-path on `order_status`; `payment_group` is only recorded. `PaymentReturn` decides from `orderStatus` + `paymentStatus` the same way for every method.
- ⚠️ **NOT handled, and it matters more now that cards are on:** refund / dispute / chargeback webhooks. The server acts on SUCCESS / FAILED / USER_DROPPED only; anything else is acknowledged and ignored, so a charged-back card payment leaves the plan active. Also still open: no on-screen confirmation if Android kills the app while the member is in their bank/UPI app (the webhook still grants the plan).
APK 17:38 (6/6 UPI schemes, NFC present). tsc 55. Not yet seen on a device with the wider methods — that needs the jar deployed first.
