### 2026-09-21 (FINAL for the day) — Cashfree checkout REWRITTEN to the React Native guide, and nothing else. ⚠️ Supersedes every Cashfree entry below it.
Client, after a day of flow experiments: "just refer this link and implement it… at the start it worked perfectly, after all these changes it is not working." The client had also rewound files by hand, which left dangling imports (`registerCashfreeCallback` called from `app/_layout.tsx` but gone from the module → **app would not open**; then the module itself missing → build error). All of that is resolved by one rewrite of **`app/(root)/utils/cashfreeCheckout.ts`** that mirrors https://www.cashfree.com/docs/payments/online/mobile/react-native ("UPI Intent Checkout") step for step:
- **Step 1** server creates the order → `userApi.createCashfreeOrder` (unchanged; server prices it and sends `payment_methods: "upi"` — deployed and verified live).
- **Step 2** `new CFSession(paymentSessionId, orderId, env)` → `new CFThemeBuilder()…build()` → `new CFUPIIntentCheckoutPayment(session, theme)` → `CFPaymentGatewayService.doUPIPayment(upiPayment)`. The callback follows the guide's sample component: **`useCashfreeCallback(navigate)`** = `setCallback` on mount, `removeCallback` on unmount. Used by `PremiumTab` (`'push'`, above its early returns) and `GatewayCheckoutView` (`'replace'`).
- **Step 3** verify from the server → `PaymentReturn` (unchanged). Its failed-state "Try again" now `router.back()`s to the plans instead of opening the old browser `/pay` page.
**REMOVED, do not resurrect without the client asking:** the `CASHFREE_CHECKOUT_FLOW` DB switch (row 30 still exists in `keyValue`, inert — nothing reads it), the `WEB` / `DROP` flows, the browser fallback, the lazy-`require` loader, and the app-start callback registration. Static imports now, exactly as the guide shows (`react-native-cashfree-pg-sdk` — the guide's sample says `…-pg-api`, a doc typo; its own install command names the sdk package). `setCallback` is a NATIVE call, so it is try/caught for a build lacking the native half.
**Facts established today that remain true:** the UPI sheet lists ONLY UPI apps installed on that phone (proved on the emulator: a dummy `upi://pay` handler + Cashfree's simulator → Android reports both); the manifest must declare the `upi/tez/gpay/phonepe/paytmmp/credpay` query schemes (done); **sandbox cannot transact with real UPI apps** — Cashfree's simulator `com.cashfree.cashfreetestupi` is the sandbox test app, so the real app list can only be judged on production keys; never `expo prebuild`; a renamed asset leaves a stale generated resource that fails `mergeReleaseResources`.
Verified: tsc 55; `npx expo export --platform android` bundles clean with `doUPIPayment` in the bundle. Needs an APK rebuild. Not yet run on a device in this form.

### 2026-09-21 — Cashfree native SDK (UPI-only Drop checkout) with the browser flow as fallback; UPI-only orders; two outcome bugs (cross-repo)
Client decision: adopt `react-native-cashfree-pg-sdk@2.4.0` BEFORE going live rather than after, and offer UPI only.

**`components/GatewayCheckoutView.tsx` now has two paths, chosen at tap time.** (1) NATIVE: `CFPaymentGatewayService.doPayment(new CFDropCheckoutPayment(session, components, theme))` with `components = CFPaymentComponentBuilder().add(CFPaymentModes.UPI)` — a native sheet of the UPI apps on the phone plus UPI id; no browser, no website `/pay` page, no deep link back (three parts that each broke during this build). (2) BROWSER: the original Custom-Tab → `/pay/:orderId` flow, kept as fallback for a build without the SDK's native half and for any SDK start failure. **The server is identical for both** — `createOrder` already returns `orderId`, `paymentSessionId`, `env`.
- ⚠️ **The SDK is loaded by lazy `require` inside try/catch, gated on `NativeModules.CashfreePgApi` — never a top-level import.** On iOS the package constructs a `NativeEventEmitter` at import, which throws when the native module is absent; a throw at import kills the whole screen instead of falling back. On Android the import succeeds but every call throws "doesn't seem to be linked". An older dev client therefore silently uses the browser path — **rebuild the dev client / APK to get the native sheet.**
- ⚠️ `setCallback` BEFORE `doPayment` (results arriving with no listener are dropped); ONE global callback, removed on unmount; both callbacks can fire, so navigation is guarded by `leftRef`.
- **`onVerify` is not proof of payment** (Cashfree's docs say so too). Both callbacks route to `PaymentReturn`, which asks OUR server, exactly as before.
- **New `cancelled` state on `PaymentReturn`** (`?cancelled=1`, sent when `onError`'s code/message contains "cancel"): one server check, and if there is no `paymentStatus` at all it says "Payment cancelled — nothing charged" immediately instead of polling 40s over an order nobody tried to pay. A PAID or terminal-failed answer still wins, and a PENDING attempt is NOT treated as cancelled.
- 💡 `cashfree-pg-api-contract` ships CommonJS code with `"type":"module"` in its package.json, so plain Node can neither `require` nor `import` it. Metro ignores that field, so the app is fine — but do not try to unit-test against it in Node without copying `dist/index.js` to a `.cjs`.

**Native config.** Android: artifacts are all on Maven Central (no repository change). The SDK adds INTERNET, ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE and **NFC** (tap-to-read CARD entry) — NFC is stripped with `tools:node="remove"` in the app manifest, same pattern as `READ_MEDIA_IMAGES`; pointless for UPI-only and it would show in the Play listing. **Verified on the freshly merged release manifest** (`merged_manifest/release/processReleaseMainManifest/` — ⚠️ NOT the stale `merged_manifests/…/processReleaseManifest/` copy from 4 Sep that sits beside it): NFC 0, Cashfree provider 1, `upi://` queries 2, `READ_MEDIA_IMAGES` and `READ_PHONE_STATE` still 0; autolinked; Metro bundles 4,447 modules clean. iOS: `LSApplicationQueriesSchemes` (15 UPI app schemes) added to Info.plist — without it iOS cannot see any installed UPI app; needs `pod install` on the next iOS build. ⚠️ **Do NOT run `npx expo prebuild`** as Cashfree's docs suggest — this project's native files are hand-maintained. `npm install` needs `--legacy-peer-deps`.

**UPI only, enforced on the server** (backend CLAUDE.md same date): `order_meta.payment_methods = "upi"` from `cashfree.payment.methods` (env `CASHFREE_PAYMENT_METHODS`, default `upi`). Set on the ORDER so the hosted page, a resumed link and any retry inherit it and a modified client cannot re-enable cards.

**🔴 Two outcome bugs found while auditing every scenario the client listed:**
- **PENDING was announced as FAILED.** App and website both did `paymentStatus !== 'SUCCESS'` after two polls (~5s). UPI collect sits at PENDING while the member approves in their UPI app — far longer — so a payment about to succeed read "Payment not completed", the message most likely to make someone pay twice. Now only `FAILED / USER_DROPPED / CANCELLED / VOID` are failures (`TERMINAL_FAILURES`, both codebases).
- **A PAID order could be stamped EXPIRED.** The 00:15 job expired on the clock with no Cashfree check: webhook missed + app closed ⇒ EXPIRED, indistinguishable from an abandoned checkout, money taken. It now calls `getOrderStatus` (which re-checks Cashfree and fulfils) for each stale ACTIVE order BEFORE `expireStale`.

⚠️ **The first release build after this failed on `mergeReleaseResources: Duplicate resources … assets_images_whatsappbg.png / .webp`** — not the SDK. The chat background was switched PNG → WebP on 2026-09-19 and the GENERATED `drawable-mdpi/assets_images_whatsappbg.png` from an earlier build was still in `android/app/build/generated/res/`; Metro only ever ADDS files there, it never removes what a renamed asset leaves behind. Third time this class has bitten (lotus logo 2026-08-10, splash). Fix = delete the stale generated file(s) under `android/app/build` (also `intermediates/packaged_res`); never `./gradlew clean` here (breaks CMake codegen, 2026-08-19). 💡 **Whenever an asset changes EXTENSION, run:** `find android/app/build/generated/res -type f | sed -E 's/\.[a-z0-9]+$//' | sort | uniq -d` — any output is a build failure waiting.

**Not verified:** the native sheet on a device (needs the rebuild), whether the Drop sheet's UPI section includes UPI-id entry on this SDK version, and that Cashfree honours `payment_methods` (local jar has no gateway credentials — confirmed only that the field lands in `order_meta`). ⚠️ The web privacy policy still says no third-party payment gateway is used — it must be updated before release, and the Play billing question from 2026-09-15 is unchanged. tsc 55.

### 2026-09-20 (later 5) — Gateway checkout: the RM screen appears because the APK has no GATEWAY support, and a PAYING member could never reach the checkout at all
Client: a Free member taps Upgrade and gets the Relationship Manager plan picker; they want Cashfree. **`PAYMENT_MODE` is already `{"mode":"GATEWAY"}` in production** and the server side is fully working — verified live: `POST /payments/cashfree/createOrder/{encodedId}` returns a real order (Silver ₹2,499 / Gold ₹4,999, `orderStatus: ACTIVE`), `https://vaibhavvivaahamatrimony.com/pay/{orderId}` answers 200, and the status poll `PaymentReturn` uses returns the plan and duration. Two reasons it still shows the callback form:
- **🔴 The installed APK predates the feature.** `components/GatewayCheckoutView.tsx` and `app/(root)/screens/PaymentReturn.tsx` are **untracked files** (`git status` → `??`) added 2026-09-15 and never built into a release. An older bundle's mode whitelist has no `'GATEWAY'` case, so it falls through to the `CONTACT` default and routes to `UpgradePlanScreen`. **Nothing in the app can fix this — it needs an APK rebuild.**
- **🔴 A member who already pays could never reach the checkout, in any build.** `buildUpgradeAction` sends every **paid** member to `UpgradePlanScreen` *without consulting the mode* (only the Free branch checks it), and that screen rendered `RelationshipManagerView` for every selection whatever the mode. So Silver → Gold had no online path even with GATEWAY set. Fixed: the screen now resolves the mode and, on GATEWAY, `router.replace`s the chosen plan into `PaymentScreen` (which renders `GatewayCheckoutView`). ⚠️ Done in an **effect**, not the render body — navigating while rendering is a side effect React warns about and can fire twice; the render returns `null` for the one frame in between. `replace`, not `push`, so returning from the browser lands where the member started rather than on a picker holding a stale selection.
- **`UpgradePlanOption` gained `id`** (`subscription_plans.id`) — the catalog mapper dropped it, and `createCashfreeOrder` needs it. ⚠️ The bundled `FALLBACK_UPGRADE_PLANS` ids are hardcoded (2/3/4/5/6, verified live) and **must match the DB**; a selection with no id falls through to the callback form rather than opening a checkout that could only fail. The server prices the order from the id and never trusts a client amount.

⚠️ **Cashfree is running in SANDBOX** (`env: sandbox` in every order response) — the flow completes but no real money moves. Production keys (`CASHFREE_ENV` / `CASHFREE_BASE_URL` / `CASHFREE_CLIENT_ID` / `CASHFREE_CLIENT_SECRET`) must be set on the server before this is a real checkout.
⚠️ The 2026-09-15 entry's warning still stands and is **not** overridden by this work: an in-app online checkout for membership is alternative billing for digital content under Google Play policy, which is why CONTACT/INFO exist. The Play enrolment decision and the privacy-policy update (it currently states no third-party payment gateway) are still open. The website flow has no such constraint.
💡 `resolvePaymentModeFast()` falls back to the **cached** mode after 700ms and to `'CONTACT'` when there is no cache — so a fresh install on a slow network still lands on the callback form for that first tap. Correct behaviour (never block a tap), worth knowing when a tester reports it once and never again.
The `/pay` page is a React shell, so the Cashfree widget mounts at runtime — **not verified without a browser**; that and the paid-member path are the two things to test on device. Probe orders for user 926 were marked EXPIRED. tsc 55. **Rebuild the APK.**

### 2026-09-20 (later 4) — 🔴 "Online" was permanent: the lastSeen endpoint forced online=true, and it is called when LEAVING
Client asked why the lastSeen API errors. **The endpoint is healthy** — 15/15 calls returned 200 in 0.4s against production, for five different members. Every failure reproduces only from a MALFORMED id: the raw numeric id gives `500 For input string: "\uFFFDn"`, `undefined` gives `500 Last unit does not have enough valid bits`. It takes the **base64** id, and all seven app call sites pass it correctly, so the errors in the log are the documented intermittent server stall (a bare timeout, no body), not a wrong call. ⚠️ `atob('926')` does **not throw** — it returns garbage ("÷n") — so `webSocketService.init`'s `catch { already decoded }` fallback is **dead code**, and a decoded id handed to `connect()` would silently open the socket as a nonsense user AND 500 every lastSeen. Guard on shape, never on atob throwing.

Looking for that, three real defects:
- **🔴 `UserConnectionService.updateLastSeen` forced `setOnline(true)`** — backwards. Three of the app's four call sites mean the member is GOING AWAY (app backgrounded, logout, socket close). So every exit re-flagged them ONLINE, and this path could never clear it. **Production had 20 members shown as "Online" whose real last-seen was hours to weeks old** (user 704 last actually seen 18 Aug, 898 on 20 Aug, 924 on 8 Sep). Worse before 2026-09-19: with nginx dropping the socket upgrade, `afterConnectionClosed` never ran at all, so nothing in the system ever wrote offline. The endpoint now records **only the timestamp**; online/offline belongs to the socket alone (`UserWebSocketHandler`: connect → true, close → false), which was already correct.
- **A server restart strands the flag too** — Tomcat force-closes every socket while the Spring context is already shutting down, so `afterConnectionClosed` logs "Skipped updateLastSeen … during shutdown" and persists nothing. **`getUserOnlineStatus` now reports the live session map** (`UserWebSocketHandler.isUserOnline`, the same source `broadcastPresence` trusts) instead of the stored flag, and heals the row when they disagree — self-correcting after every deploy.
- **🔴 Chat header showed "last seen" 5h30m early.** `formatLastSeenTime` fed the REST value straight to `new Date()`. The server sends UTC with **no zone** (`2026-09-20T06:38:53.226174`), which JS reads as LOCAL — 11:53 AM rendered as 6:23 AM. Exactly the chat-LIST bug fixed 2026-09-19; missed here because that entry only mentioned message timestamps. Now the same zone-aware parse.
- ⚠️ **`broadcastPresence` was the other half**, and the two disagreed: it sent `LocalDateTime.now()` — also unmarked, but in the server's LOCAL time — so one parser could not be right for both sources. It now sends `Instant.now()` (ends in `Z`), and the app only adds a marker when there is none.

**Verified end-to-end on the new jar** (no socket → offline · lastSeen while away → stays offline · socket opens → online · socket closes → offline) and the timestamp **against production**: rendered 12:08:53 IST against a 12:08:52 wall clock, 1s skew. 💡 The render depends on the production JVM running UTC — it does; my local JVM is IST and the same value came back unconverted there, which is why this had to be checked on production, not locally.

**Data:** the 20 stale flags were cleared in production (backup `_bak_users_isonline_20260920`, userId + isOnline + lastSeen). ⚠️ **Until the jar is deployed the old endpoint keeps re-flagging members online on every exit**, so expect the count to creep back up before the deploy; re-run `UPDATE users SET isOnline=0 WHERE isOnline=1;` once afterwards. ⚠️ My probes overwrote `lastSeen` for 926/928/931/706/707 (all test/demo accounts) — transient data, overwritten by normal use.

⚠️ `users.lastSeen` stores IST digits while MySQL's own `NOW()` is UTC, so raw SQL like `TIMESTAMPDIFF(...,NOW())` reads 5h30m out. The JDBC round-trip is self-consistent (the API returns true UTC), so this is an analysis trap only — not worth a column migration. tsc 55. **Deploy the jar** (`target/app.jar`, clean, 12:01) — the app change rides the APK.

### 2026-09-20 (later 3) — 🔴 A plan change needed an app restart to take effect: one `refreshSubscription()`, seven triggers, a server `plan_changed` signal (cross-repo)
Client: after an admin changes a plan (and soon, after a gateway payment) the new features only work after logout/login or killing the app. Four causes, all real:
- **Only Home ever fetched the plan, once, inside its `loadData`.** Nothing else in the app asked again, so a change was invisible until Home was rebuilt. ⚠️ This file documented a `refreshSubscription` on the context for months — **it never existed in the code.**
- `PaymentReturn` showed "your plan is now active" and sent the member to Home still holding the old plan in memory.
- The server told nobody. All plan changes already funnel through `SubscriptionActivationService.activatePlan`, which sent a push + email but no live signal.
- **🔴 Home wrote `planTitle: 'Free'` on ANY failed request** — a network blip downgraded a paying member until the next good load. Same trap in the unused `masterService.loadUserSubscription` (made safe, marked deprecated).

**The rule, same as chat sync: the REST answer is the only source of truth; everything else is a signal to ask again.** New `refreshSubscription(reason, force)` in `subscriptionContext.tsx`: 200 → set, **404 → Free, anything else (500 / network) → keep what we have**; bursts coalesced (one in flight + one queued re-run, the caller's promise includes the re-run); 3s throttle unless `force`; an answer that lands after logout/account switch is dropped (`userIdRef` check). Triggers, all the same function: member becomes known (app start / login) · app foreground · socket `connected` · socket **`plan_changed`** (forced) · any push received or tapped · any business **403** (axios interceptor) · `PaymentReturn` on paid (forced) · Home load.
- Non-React callers (axios interceptor, push hook) go through the import-free **`utils/subscriptionRefreshBus.ts`**. ⚠️ It must stay import-free: context → userApi → axiosClient → context would be a cycle.
- ⚠️ The 403 trigger **excludes the `userSubscriptions` URL** — a 403 from the plan endpoint itself would re-trigger the refresh that made the call, and the run-once-more rule would loop forever.
- **Plan-change toast** ("Your plan is now Gold") via `react-native-alert-notification`'s `Toast` — the one actually mounted in `_layout`; deliberately NOT `popup.*` (stacked-Modal freeze, §17). Shown only when the previous plan was fetched for the SAME member in this run (`planOwnerRef`), so login, cold start and account switching never announce anything.
- **`PaymentReturn`**: on `paid` the button reads "Unlocking your features…" and stays disabled while it force-refreshes until the held plan matches the one bought (the order can read PAID a beat before the webhook writes the row). Bounded at 6 tries, then releases regardless — never trap someone who has paid.
- **Home**: both of its plan fetches are deleted; `userTier` is now DERIVED from `subscriptionData.planTitle` instead of being private state, so the header chip follows a live change.

**Backend** (detail in its CLAUDE.md): `SubscriptionActivationService.notifyPlanChanged()` sends `{"type":"plan_changed"}` from activatePlan (admin approve, admin assign, Cashfree webhook), the nightly expiry and the direct-create endpoint. **Commit-aware** — inside a transaction it defers to `afterCommit`, otherwise the app re-reads the OLD plan.

**Verified on the new jar against the production DB:** member 926 holding an open socket heard `plan_changed` 1.2s after an admin assign — 2s BEFORE the admin call returned — and an immediate re-read already showed the new row. Test row + notification removed afterwards. tsc 55. **Not yet device-tested; the transactional `approvePayment` path is covered by the same helper but was not exercised live.**

**Step 4, done same day — and the premise was half wrong, worth recording.** The note that stood here said `SearchTabs` / `SearchResult` / `chatscreen` / `ProfileDetail` gate on private plan copies set once on mount. **They do not**: each already recomputes its flag in an effect keyed on `subscriptionData`, so locks and buttons always followed a live change. What did NOT follow is **data the server masked under the old plan** — a profile's mobile / email / horoscope / birth time, a chat thread, the chat-list previews ("🔒 upgrade to read"), the usage counters. An upgrade therefore produced unlocked buttons over null data until the screen was left and re-entered. 💡 When a plan change "doesn't take", ask first whether it is the GATE or the DATA that is stale.
New **`usePlanChangeEffect(cb)`** (exported from `subscriptionContext.tsx`): fires when `subscriptionId|planTitle` changes WHILE the screen is mounted — never for the first value a screen sees, so cold start (empty → restored → confirmed) is not a "change"; a same-tier renewal IS (new row, counters reset). Wired into:
- **`ProfileDetail`** — re-runs `loadAll` via a `planReloadKey` dep. SILENT (`silentReloadRef`): no full-page skeleton over a profile already on screen, and no second `viewedProfile` hit for the same visit.
- **`chatscreen`** — `sync()`. The wall lifts by itself; the cursor is still 0 for a member who was walled, so that one call is the full history.
- **`myChatList`** — refetch. The tab never unmounts, so masked previews would otherwise sit there until a new message patched a row.
- **`(tabs)/profile`** — `refreshStats()` (requests left, boost credits), beating its 30s focus throttle.
Deliberately NOT wired: `SearchResult` (results fetched under the old plan are still valid rows; re-running a search is the member's call). ⚠️ `usePlanChangeEffect` is a hook — keep it above every early return (`chatscreen` has one at the premium wall; checked for all four). **Any new screen that renders server-masked data needs this hook.** **Deploy the jar before the APK.**

### 2026-09-20 — Name fields accept Tamil (and any other script)
Client: members must be able to register and edit their names in Tamil. Every name input silently **ate the characters as they were typed** — `text.replace(/[^A-Za-z\s]/g,'')` in `sign-up.tsx` (First/Last Name, and Occupation, which shares the same helper) and in `EditProfileSection.tsx` (First/Last/Fathers/Mothers Name via each field's `nameRules` flag). Nothing was rejected or explained; the box just stayed empty, so it read as a broken keyboard rather than a validation rule.

Both now call one shared `stripNonNameChars()` in `constants/data.ts` — letters of **any** script plus spaces, with digits, punctuation and emoji still stripped as they are typed. Leading-space/collapse/max-length and the 2-character minimum are unchanged.

⚠️ **`\p{M}` is load-bearing, `\p{L}` alone is not enough.** Tamil vowel signs and the virama (ா ி ு ்) are COMBINING MARKS, not letters, so a letters-only filter mangles every word — "முருகன்" becomes "மரகன". Any future "letters only" rule on this data needs both classes.

⚠️ The regex is built with `new RegExp(...)` inside a try/catch, not as a literal. Unicode property escapes are a **syntax error** on an engine that lacks them, which throws at module load and takes the whole screen down rather than failing at call time; the catch falls back to stripping ASCII digits and punctuation. Hermes on RN 0.81 supports them, so the fallback should never fire.

**Verified against production, not just compiled:** no server-side name validation exists (the only `@Pattern` DTO rules are mobile and OTP), and a Tamil first + last name saved through `update-personal-info` stored correctly and came back intact from `getUserByUserId`; a Tamil father's name round-tripped through `update-family-info` too. The columns were already `utf8mb4`. Test values restored afterwards.

💡 Name **search** is unaffected — `filterUsers` never matches on name (only profile id) — but a member who stores their name in Tamil is findable only by someone typing Tamil, should name search ever be added. tsc 55 unchanged. Rides the next APK; no backend change.

⚠️ One `[^A-Za-z\s]` filter remains, in the **retired** `components/editProfileModal.tsx`. Left alone deliberately: it is reachable only via `components/tabs.tsx` → `profile_backup.tsx`, which nothing imports. If that chain is ever revived, it needs this same fix.

### 2026-09-20 (later 2) — Notification icon redrawn: the line-art lotus was ~10% ink and unreadable in the shade
Client: pushes arrive, but the icon in the pulled-down shade "is not clearly showing". The plumbing from 2026-08-13 was all still correct (white-on-transparent drawables in 5 densities, both `expo.modules.notifications.*` and `com.google.firebase.messaging.*` meta-data, tint `#420001`) — the **artwork** was the problem, exactly the limit flagged in that entry: the glyph was derived from the intricate multi-petal LOGO line-art, measured at **~10.6% solid pixels, filling only ~77% × 40% of its canvas**. Android draws the small icon at ~16–24dp, where that is a few faint hairlines.
Replaced with a **purpose-drawn glyph**: five SOLID pointed petals (outer ±58°, mid ±29°, tall centre) over a short ground bar, each petal cut out with a thin transparent moat so they still read as separate petals when filled. Rendered once at 1536px and LANCZOS-downsampled to every density → **~36% solid, ~90% × 87% of the canvas** at all five sizes; previewed tinted at true 24px and it still reads as a lotus. `assets/images/notification-icon.png` (the app.json plugin source) regenerated from the same master so a future `expo prebuild` produces the same thing.
⚠️ Drawables are native resources — **needs an APK rebuild**, and a notification already sitting in the shade keeps the old icon until a new one arrives. Generator kept in the session scratchpad only; to redraw, regenerate all five `drawable-*/notification_icon.png` + the assets copy together from ONE master, never resize them individually from each other.

### 2026-09-20 (later) — 🔴 Native pickers were invisible in dark mode: AppTheme was DayNight while the app is light-only
Client: the new birth-time picker scrolls and selects fine but **the numbers are not visible**. Root cause is not that picker — **`android/app/src/main/res/values/styles.xml` had `AppTheme` on `Theme.AppCompat.DayNight.NoActionBar`**, so on a phone with system dark mode ON every NATIVE dialog (date picker, time picker, alerts) rendered with dark-mode colours while the app around them — declared `"userInterfaceStyle": "light"` in app.json and light-themed on every screen — stayed white. Result: light text on a white sheet. Now `Theme.AppCompat.Light.NoActionBar`, matching what app.json already declares. Checked before changing: `values-night/` contains ONLY `splashscreen_background`, the same `#420001` as the day value, so nothing else shifts.
- **This fixes every native picker at once**, not just birth time: sign-up's DOB picker, EditProfileSection's DOB picker, StarMatch's date picker and any native alert had the same exposure for dark-mode users.
- Belt and braces on the two iOS spinners in EditProfileSection (`themeVariant="light"` + `textColor="#0f1724"`), since their sheet is explicitly white — iOS would otherwise render white-on-white regardless of the app theme.
Both iOS picker sheets also gained a **header with a close (×) button** and **tap-outside-to-dismiss** (client): the backdrop is a `TouchableOpacity` that closes on press, and the sheet is wrapped in a second one with `activeOpacity={1}` + a no-op `onPress`, so a tap ON the sheet cannot bubble up and dismiss the picker mid-scroll. Dismissing either way makes NO change — only Confirm commits. Android keeps the OS dialog, where cancel/outside-tap are handled by the system and arrive as a non-`'set'` event that is already ignored.
⚠️ **Native change — needs a rebuild**, not a JS reload. StarMatch's own custom wheel is unaffected: it is a hand-built ScrollView list on a deliberately dark sheet with cream text, not a native picker.
tsc 55.

### 2026-09-20 — Birth time across the app (my profile · edit · completion · profile detail · privacy)
Stored in `astroInfo[0].birth_time` (backend CLAUDE.md same date). Five surfaces:
- **My profile → Religious**: new "Birth time" row. ⚠️ Dosham lost its `full` so the two pair up — a full-width Dosham left the cell beside it empty.
- **Edit → Religious Details**: a **time picker**, the same control Star Match uses for time of birth (client), via a new `kind: 'time'` on the field spec — Android's inline dialog, an iOS sheet with Confirm, mirroring the existing `'date'` branch and reusing its `overlay`/`dateSheet`/`dateConfirm` styles. Stores `moment(d).format('hh:mm A')` → `"10:45 PM"`, so the value is always parseable for horoscope matching instead of whatever free text a member typed. ⚠️ Opening it parses the CURRENT value **non-strictly** across `hh:mm A / h:mm A / HH:mm / H:mm`, so anything an earlier build saved still opens the wheel at the right time and displays normalised (`22:45` → `10:45 PM`, `morning 6.30` → `06:30 AM`); an unparseable value is shown verbatim rather than lost. New optional `hint` on the field spec renders **in the error row's slot** while there is no error, so adding one never changes a field's height (this form is deliberately fixed-height).
- **Home profile-completion ring** picks it up automatically — the percentage comes from the backend, which now counts it (35 → 36 fields).
- **Profile detail → Religious Details**: shows "Birth Time", revealed by the SAME tap as contact + horoscope (`revealedBirthTime` from `revealContact`). Server masks it otherwise, so an empty value means "not revealed yet", not "not set".
- **Privacy settings**: new "Birth Time" toggle (`hidden_fields.fieldName = 'birthTime'`). When hidden, profile detail renders the standard `RestrictedField` ask-permission control (`fieldType="BIRTH_TIME"`) instead of the value, and `permissionRequests.birthTime` tracks a pending request — same pattern as Mobile/Horoscope/Profile Image.
tsc 55. **Deploy the jar first** — without it the field cannot be saved or revealed.

### 2026-09-19 (later 7) — 🔴 nginx was dropping the WebSocket upgrade: live chat had NEVER worked in production; chat-list time was 5h30m out; chat background load
- **🔴 THE root cause behind "notification arrives, message doesn't".** With the new jar deployed, a WS handshake through the public URL returned **400** while the SAME jar answered **101** on port 9100 directly, and locally — i.e. the app and the jar were always fine, **nginx was stripping the `Upgrade` header**, so no client had ever held a live socket in production. Every message only ever arrived by push (Expo, which never touches the socket) and the chat refreshed only on reopen. Fixed in the server's nginx config (no code, no rebuild): `proxy_http_version 1.1`, `proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade`, `proxy_set_header Connection $connection_upgrade` + the `map $http_upgrade $connection_upgrade` block in `http {}`, and `proxy_read_timeout 3600s` (without it idle sockets die at 60s and every client reconnects each minute). ⚠️ **Any server rebuild/migration must re-apply this** — the app degrades silently, with no error, straight back to "no live chat". Verified live afterwards: handshake 101 through nginx AND Cloudflare; heartbeat pong; a message reaching an open chat; `{"delivered":true}` at send; `message_read` back to the sender; a second socket for the same user taking over (the session-removal fix); `presence` pushed to the partner. Cloudflare was never the problem — WS is on by default there.
- **🔴 Chat-list timestamps were 5h30m wrong** (10:03 pm shown as 4:33 pm). `/conversation/chatlist` serialises `LocalDateTime` with **no zone** (`2026-09-19T16:33:14.687`) and `formatRelativeChatTime` fed that straight to `new Date()`, which reads an unmarked string as LOCAL — so UTC was treated as IST. chatscreen's `formatDate` had always appended `Z`; this screen never did. Now it appends `Z` only when there is no zone, because live socket events DO carry one (`…707536959Z`) — verified in node across all three shapes plus garbage input. This also fixes the "Yesterday"/weekday labels, which were computed from the same wrong instant and could name the wrong day for anything after 18:30 IST.
- **Conversation list, two live-update gaps:** a patched row kept its old POSITION (a new message updated the preview while the row stayed buried) — it now moves to the top like WhatsApp; and the tab keeps FOCUS while the app is backgrounded, so returning to a slept phone never re-ran the focus fetch — an `AppState` 'active' trigger now calls `ensureConnected()` + refetch, matching chatscreen.
- **Chat background: 682 KB PNG → 54 KB WebP (92% smaller), pixel-wise indistinguishable** (RMSE 1.22/255; it is fully OPAQUE, so the PNG's alpha channel was pure waste, and only 48 distinct colours). The perceived delay was mostly RN's **default 300ms image fade** — now `fadeDuration={0}`, with the pattern's own dominant shade (`#E5DDD5`, 67% of its pixels) as the container background so the chat paints the right colour on the first frame instead of flashing blank. PNG deleted; the .webp is the only copy.
tsc 55. Rides the next APK (the nginx fix is already live and benefits the CURRENT build too).

### 2026-09-19 (later 6) — Chat screen rebuilt around ONE `sync()`; ⋮ menu row alignment
**The rule (keep it):** the server's REST API is the only source of message data; the WebSocket merely says "something changed". `chatscreen.tsx` had FOUR copy-pasted blocks that each re-downloaded the entire conversation and swapped the list (on open, on a live message, on a receipt, after sending). All four are replaced by one `sync()`:
- asks `userApi.syncConversation(conversationId, me, afterId)` for rows newer than the newest CONFIRMED id it holds (`afterId` ignores optimistic rows — they carry a `Date.now()` id), **appends** them de-duplicated by id, and applies `readUpToId` / `deliveredUpToId` to its own older rows so ticks still move. It only allocates a new message object when a tick actually changes, so memoised bubbles don't re-render on every sync.
- **Triggers — all the same function:** screen open (`afterId 0`), `chat_message`, `message_read`, `message_delivered`, the socket's `connected` (catch-up after a drop), **app returning to foreground** (`AppState` → `ensureConnected()` + `sync()`; the socket may have been dead the whole time) and our own send. A missed or repeated socket event is therefore harmless.
- Bursts are coalesced (`syncingRef` + one queued re-run via `syncRef`), and `messagesRef` gives the callback the latest list without a stale closure.
- **Sending:** the optimistic row is `pending: true` (clock icon, like WhatsApp) and is CONFIRMED IN PLACE from the send response (`{id, delivered}`) instead of being thrown away by a re-download. ⚠️ Because it is now kept, it must carry `displayDateGroup: 'Today'` — undefined never equals the row below it, which drew a stray date separator above every sent message. That also fixed a long-standing type error on that line → **tsc baseline is now 55**.
- `getConversationData` has no callers left in this screen (still used nowhere else; left in `userApi` for old builds' contract clarity).
**ProfileDetail ⋮ menu:** "Ends the match and closes the chat." ran past the menu's edge — the text column had no `flex: 1`, so it was unbounded (154px available, ~175 needed). Shared `menuRow` / `menuTextCol` / `menuSub` styles now bound the column so it wraps, pin the icon to the title line (a centred icon drifted on the one row that wraps), and the menu is 236 wide. All three rows use them.
**Next (agreed order):** an automated two-user chat test against the server, then client message ids + safe retry, then a "Reconnecting…" strip. Deploy the 21:43 jar before this APK for the full behaviour; against an older server `sync()` accepts the legacy full array (chat still works, just without incremental fetch or tick high-water marks) rather than rendering an empty thread.

### 2026-09-19 (later 4) — Reveal Contact told a Trial member to "upgrade" when the real blocker was no accepted interest
Client (Trial pack, 5 reveals): tapped **Reveal Contact & Horoscope** on a profile they had not sent an interest to, and got "Upgrade your plan to view contact details". The server was right — `revealContact` answers **403 `INTEREST_NOT_APPROVED`** for an unconnected pair (and spends no reveal) — but `handleRevealContact` only special-cased `CONTACT_VIEW_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`, so this consent refusal fell into the generic `code === 403` branch and was presented as a PLAN refusal to someone who already has reveals. New `showNotConnected()` in `InlineProfileTabs` words the three real states: no interest / declined → **"Send an interest first"**; PENDING sent by the viewer → **"Waiting for acceptance"** (they must accept your request); PENDING sent by the other member → **"Accept their interest first"**. It runs **before** the API call when `interestStatus !== 'APPROVED'` (known locally — no round trip just to be refused) and again on the server's `INTEREST_NOT_APPROVED` as the fallback for a stale local status. `isSender` is now passed into `InlineProfileTabs`; it is reliable (set from `interactionStatus.interest.sentBy === 'VIEWER'` on load, `true` after sending, reset on unfriend). 💡 Same lesson as the HTTP-200 landmine: **a 403 here has three different meanings (plan, quota, consent) — branch on `message`, not on `code`.** tsc 56. Rides the next APK; no backend change.

### 2026-09-19 (later 5) — 🔴 Live messages never appeared inside an open chat; WhatsApp ticks + presence; chat list rebuilt for speed; block popups; horoscope zoom; N/A → "-"
**🔴 "Both online, I'm in the chat, nothing appears — the notification does."** Three causes, one here and two on the server (backend CLAUDE.md same date):
- **`webSocketService` kept ONE listener per message type** (`Map<string, callback>`). The chat list is a tab that never unmounts and listens to `chat_message`; so does an open chat. Each registration overwrote the other's, and `removeChatListener()` took no argument — it deleted whatever was registered. The list unregisters on BLUR, which can run *after* the chat screen has registered on mount → it deleted the open chat's listener. Now a `Set` per type; `addListener`/`addChatListener` **return an unsubscribe**, and `removeListener(type)` without a callback deliberately does nothing. ⚠️ Every effect must clean up with the returned function — never reintroduce a remove-all.
- **Reconnect gave up after 5 tries (~45s)** and there was no heartbeat, so a phone that slept — or any backend redeploy — left the app deaf until restart. Now unlimited with a 30s backoff cap, a 25s `ping` / 60s silence watchdog, `ensureConnected()` on app foreground, and a `connected` event that open screens use to refetch what they missed. A pending reconnect timer no longer tears down a socket that `connect()` already brought up.
- (`AuthContext`'s dead TOP provider shares the interface name, so TS merges the two declarations — both had to change.)

**Ticks like WhatsApp** — `✓` sent · `✓✓` grey delivered to their device · `✓✓` blue read (`isDelivered`, with read implying delivered for legacy rows). `message_delivered`, `message_read` and the socket's own `connected` all trigger the same refetch. **Presence is pushed now** (`presence` event → Online / last seen flips instantly); the 15s poll stays as a fallback.

**Chat list, 100 conversations (client: scrolling not smooth).** The cost was MOUNTING rows, not re-rendering them — with `removeClippedSubviews` + a render window, rows are built and destroyed continuously, and each one constructed a NativeBase `Pressable` + `Avatar` + three `Text`s (theme + style-prop resolution per mount), a `LinearGradient` just to draw a 2.5px ring, and fresh inline style objects. `components/listchats.js` rewritten on plain RN primitives + `StyleSheet`, solid ring, **fixed 84px rows + `getItemLayout`** (no measuring while flinging; ⚠️ the border is ALWAYS 1px, transparent unless unread — a border only on unread rows changed their height and breaks the offsets), `fadeDuration={0}` avatars, search derived with `useMemo` instead of two mirrored state copies. `myChatList.handleChatPress` is now `useCallback` — as a plain function it changed identity every render, so ONE incoming message re-rendered all 100 memoised rows.
**Delete removed from the conversation list** (client) — selection mode, long-press, the ⋮ "Select" menu and its `MenuProvider` are gone (also retiring a §17 stranded-backdrop risk from a persistent tab). `userApi.deleteConversation` remains, unused.

**Blocked chats (client decision, reverses 09-17's silent block):** both members can open the chat and read history. Blocker sends → `popup.confirm('Unblock to send', …, Unblock / Cancel)`; **Unblock now keeps you in the chat** (it used to bounce to the list, discarding the typed message). Blocked member sends → "… has blocked you, so you can't message them." The server answers the same two cases (`USER_BLOCKED` / `BLOCKED_BY_USER`) for a block that lands while the screen is open.

**Horoscope: pinch / double-tap / drag zoom** (up to 5×, pan clamped) in the full-screen viewer — a chart is dense small print and "expand" alone left it unreadable. ⚠️ The Modal wraps its content in its OWN `GestureHandlerRootView`: a Modal is a separate native window outside the app-wide root, and gestures inside it do nothing on Android without one. This is not the §17 violation — that rule is about a second full-screen root over the MAIN tree; this one lives and dies with the Modal.
**"N/A" → "-"** on search-result cards, the discovery grid and the contact reveal; `ProfileDetail.isBlankValue` also treats `n/a` / `not specified` arriving as DATA as blank.
tsc 56. **Deploy the backend jar FIRST** (delivered ticks, presence and the block answers need it; the app degrades to the old two-state ticks without it), then the APK.

### 2026-09-19 (later 4) — Flow audit of photo-expand + contact visibility across plans; 🔴 expand button bypassed the owner's photo privacy
Client asked for a flow check. Rules verified in code AND against live production with real accepted pairs (Silver 931→Gold 928, Trial 926→Gold 928, Gold 704→Platinum 707, plus no-interest and Free probes):
- **Photo expand** — blocked ONLY for Free (`!planTitle || planTitle === 'Free'`); Trial/Starter/Classic/Silver/Gold/Platinum all open it. The VIEWED member's plan is irrelevant, so Gold↔Silver works both ways.
- **Contact** — decided by the VIEWER's plan (server, `getProfileDetailWithIntractionStatus`): Silver+ (`enabled`) with an ACCEPTED interest → full mobile + email, no reveal step (Silver→Gold confirmed live); same plans WITHOUT an accepted interest → `•••••• 1234`, no email; quota plans (Classic/Trial) → null until they spend a reveal, then the same interest rule; Free → always null. Horoscope = plan has `HOROSCOPE_VIEW` AND interest accepted.
- **🔴 Found + fixed: the expand button ignored the owner's "hide my photo" setting.** The hero correctly blurs and shows "Ask Permission" when `profileImage` is hidden and this viewer is not approved — but the expand button sits outside that branch and `openImageModal` checked only the viewer's PLAN, so any paid member could open the full-screen viewer and see the real photo plus the entire gallery. New single `photoRestricted` flag (shared with the hero branch so they cannot drift) now stops `openImageModal` first — "Photos are private… tap Ask Permission" — and dims the button. Privacy first, plan second.
- ⚠️ **Not fixed, flagged (security bucket):** the server never consults `hidden_fields` / `restricted_field_requests` — `UserService` has zero references — so a hidden mobile/photo/horoscope is still PRESENT in the API response and only the app hides it. Correct for app users, but readable by anyone inspecting traffic. Belongs with the deferred auth/IDOR work in `AUDIT_REPORT.md`.
tsc 56. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-19 (later 3) — Trial members got the upgrade wall instead of their 5 contact reveals; profile Photos card under the footer
- **🔴 A Trial member saw "Upgrade to view contact details" (and the horoscope wall) on ProfileDetail** even though the pack carries **5 contact reveals** (`VIEW_PERSONAL_INFO = 5`, alongside its 25 requests). The "Reveal Contact & Horoscope" button was gated on **`planTitle === 'Classic'`** — a hardcoded plan name (the §17 rule, broken in gating rather than copy) — so every other quota plan fell through to the flat wall. Verified live: Trial's entitlement is `viewPersonalInfo: {period: PLAN_DURATION, limit: 5}`, the same `{period, limit}` object shape Classic gets; Silver+ get `true`. New module-level `isQuotaEntitlement(v)` (object → quota) feeds a `hasRevealQuota` state resolved in all three places `isPremiumValue` is, passed into `InlineProfileTabs`, and used for BOTH the Contact and the Horoscope reveal branches. Any future pack with a numeric reveal quota now works without an app change. **Exhausted quota** (backend `CONTACT_VIEW_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`, which returns `{limit, used}`) now says **"You have used all 5 of your contact reveals in this plan"** with the upgrade action, and sets the on-screen counter to 0 of N.
- **Profile page: the Photos card's bottom row sat behind the footer.** The ScrollView padded a flat `80`, but the footer is `68 + max(insets.bottom, 14) + 4` (~120 on a gesture-nav phone). Now `useFooterClearance()` — the same hook mailBox already uses, so the two cannot drift.
tsc 56. Rides the next APK; no backend change.

### 2026-09-19 (later 2) — "Remove Connection" → "Unfriend"; locked Call/Match buttons show an ⓘ instead of a sentence
- **Unfriend** is now the word everywhere the client sees it: ProfileDetail ⋮ menu, its confirm (`Unfriend <name>?`), success ("Unfriended"), and both mailBox confirms (Received-tab accepted rows, Sent-tab approved rows) with an **Unfriend** button. Copy also states the rule plainly: earlier messages are kept; only a chat where nothing was ever said is removed. `userApi.removeConnection` / `DELETE /mailbox/removeConnection` are unchanged — the endpoint name is a contract, the label is not.
- **Request Call / Match Score, when locked behind "accept your interest":** the full sentence under each button ("Available after they accept your interest" — wrapping to two or three lines in a half-width button) is replaced by a small **ⓘ inline right after the label** (client: next to it, not on a line below) that opens the explanation on tap. ⚠️ The buttons are **no longer `disabled`** in the locked state — a disabled parent `TouchableOpacity` swallows every touch in its subtree, so an icon inside one can never be tapped. They keep the greyed styling, and their own `onPress` (`handleRequestCall` / `handleStarMatch`) already answers this exact state with `popup.info('Not connected yet', …)`; the ⓘ calls the same handler, so the copy has one source. Orphaned `secondaryBtnSubtext` style deleted. tsc 56. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-19 (later) — Unfriend error was a DB enum; name edits now reach the home header; Personal card shows name + contact
- **🔴 "Something went wrong" on Remove Connection was `Data truncated for column 'status'`** — the `DISCONNECTED` value added on 09-17 was never added to the live `conversations.status` MySQL ENUM, and `ddl-auto=update` does not extend an enum. **DB-only fix, already applied to production** (see backend CLAUDE.md 2026-09-19 + its new §17 landmine): ALTER + repair of the 2 conversations left stuck `ACCEPTED`. **Chat history is never deleted on unfriend** — a thread with real messages is kept and marked DISCONNECTED; only a thread containing nothing but the two auto-generated system lines is removed, so no empty ghost threads pile up. Verified live: sending to a disconnected thread returns 403, both threads stay listed, all 12 messages still readable.
- **Disconnected-thread popup reworded to the client's ask** — "Send an interest first to start a conversation with X. Your earlier messages are kept here." with **View Profile** (→ `ProfileDetail`, raw numeric id per §17) / Close.
- **🔴 Editing your name did not update the home header.** `refreshProfile` synced **only** `profileImage` back into `UserDataContext` (which is why the photo worked and the name did not — the header reads `userData.firstName/lastName`, filled from AsyncStorage once at login). It now syncs first/last name from the same authoritative server response. ⚠️ `userData.firstName/lastName` had to be ADDED to that `useCallback`'s deps, or the closure keeps the pre-edit name and rewrites the same value on every refresh; safe because nothing has `refreshProfile` in an effect dependency array.
- **Personal card now shows First name, Last name, Mobile and Email** (Email full-width for long addresses; **Physical status** moved up from its own full row into the cell beside Mobile, so no cell in the 2-column grid is left blank — client's choice over a half-width Email) — name was editable but never displayed, and contact details were nowhere on the own-profile page (client). **Mobile and Email are read-only everywhere**: they are the two login identities (and the number the team calls to verify a profile), so they are a support-desk change. They also render in the edit page as locked rows with "Contact support to change" — the same treatment Gender/Caste already use — so a member can see them without hunting instead of the fields simply being absent. `validate()` and the `isDirty` baseline both already skip readonly fields, so neither is affected.
tsc 56 unchanged. **App changes ride the next APK; the unfriend fix is live already (DB only, no deploy).**

### 2026-09-19 — Trial chip STILL read "Free" (second cause); mother tongue printed raw JSON; login stops naming which half was wrong
- **🔴 Trial member's home chip read "Free" again — a SECOND cause, upstream of the one fixed on 2026-09-05.** That fix taught `VVMWelcomeHeader.resolveTier` to pass an unknown-but-real plan name through as itself; it just never received one. `(tabs)/index.tsx`'s own `getTierStyle` runs first, switches on six hardcoded tier codes, and its `default:` branch returned the literal **`name: 'Free'`** — so `TRIAL` (and any future campaign tier) was rewritten to "Free" before `resolveTier` ever saw it. `tierStyle.name` is the only field of that object anyone reads (`tierName={tierStyle?.name}`). Now the default keeps the real name for anything that isn't free/empty, and `setUserTier` is fed **`planTitle` ("Trial") rather than `planCode` ("TRIAL")** — the switch uppercases before matching, so the six named tiers resolve identically either way. Verified live: `/userSubscriptions/getActiveUserSubscriptionByUserId/926` already returned `planTitle: "Trial"` with the correct Trial entitlements, i.e. the server was never the problem. 💡 When a "show X, not the fallback" fix does not take, check whether an earlier caller already collapsed X into the fallback.
- **Mother tongue rendered `["Tamil", "English"]`** — `user_details.languages` is a **JSON column**, so the API hands over that raw text and both profile screens printed it verbatim. New shared **`formatLanguageList()`** in `constants/data.ts` (JSON string, real array or comma text → `Tamil, English`; capitalises each entry because the stored casing is inconsistent — 'tamil' vs 'Tamil'; empty/`[]`/`null` → `''`; malformed JSON is stripped of its brackets rather than shown). Wired into `ProfileDetail`'s Mother Tongue row (now prefers `basicInfo.mother_language`, falling back to `languages`) and `profile.tsx`'s. ⚠️ Deliberately NOT applied to `formatUserDetails`'s `PersonalDetail` map — that feeds the **edit page's** `Mother Language` picker, which would then be handed a joined "Tamil, English" that is not one of its options.
- **Login no longer says which half was wrong.** 404 ("Not Registered — no account found…") and 401 ("Invalid PIN — the PIN you entered is incorrect") are now ONE popup: **"Invalid Credentials — The mobile number / email or PIN you entered is incorrect."** Client ask, and the standard practice: a distinct "Not Registered" reply confirms which mobiles and emails have accounts here, so anyone could enumerate members one guess at a time. The 403 branch (PENDING/REJECTED → ProfileUnderVerificationScreen, BANNED/SUSPENDED → "Account Blocked") is unchanged.
tsc 56 unchanged. **App-only — no backend change, so this rides the next APK and needs no deploy.**

### 2026-09-17 — Request Call now notifies the member being called (cross-repo)
Client: the person receiving a call request should be told too. Backend `ServiceRequestService.createRequest` saved the `ServiceRequest` row and notified **nobody** — only the admin queue ever saw it, so the target member had no idea a call was coming. Now, for `VOICE_CALL` with a `targetUserId` (which `ProfileDetail.handleRequestCall` already sends), a new `notifyCallTarget()` fires **after** the save: push via `sendPushNotificationToUserDirect` (essential, not plan-gated — same reasoning as interest-received) with the requester's name through `nameForRecipient` (Free recipients see "Someone"), plus an in-app `Notification` row, category **`CALL_REQUEST`**. Best-effort in its own try/catch — a notification failure must never turn an already-saved request into an error for the requester. App: `NotificationCard` gained a `call_request` case (green `Phone` icon) so it doesn't fall to the grey default eye; the screen passes `notificationCategory` straight through and lowercases it. Backend `./mvnw compile` clean; app tsc 56. **Needs backend deploy** (the app change is icon-only).

### 2026-09-17 (later) — Connections & chat: mutual-interest sync, unfriend from either side, WhatsApp-style block, free-member previews (cross-repo)
Six client items on the interest/chat flow. Backend detail in the backend CLAUDE.md same date.
- **Mutual interests now resolve as ONE state.** Two members can each send the other an interest (two rows, one per direction). Accepting either now also flips the reverse row to APPROVED server-side, and **sending an interest to someone whose interest is already pending on you is treated as accepting it** (server answers 201 with `acceptStatus: APPROVED`; `ProfileDetail` shows "It's a match!" and goes straight to Chat Now instead of "Request Pending"). No more Accepted-on-one-screen / Declined-on-the-other.
- **Remove connection from EITHER side.** New `userApi.removeConnection(encodedUserId, otherUserId)` → `DELETE /mailbox/removeConnection/…` deletes BOTH interest rows. Wired to a red person-remove icon on **accepted rows of the Received tab** (the receiver had no way to undo before — only Sent By You had an X) and to a **"Remove Connection" entry in ProfileDetail's ⋮ menu** when connected. ⚠️ The old per-id `deleteInterestRequest` also removes the reverse row now — deleting one row of a mutual pair left the other APPROVED, so `findBetweenUsers` still said "connected".
- **After unfriend the chat cannot be opened or written.** Conversation status `DISCONNECTED` (new `ChatStatus`, history kept). `myChatList` shows a "Not connected → View Profile" popup instead of opening; `chatscreen` blocks send with the status modal; the server refuses with 403 `NOT_CONNECTED` (and `REQUEST_PENDING` for pending threads — that was client-only before). A re-sent + accepted interest re-opens it.
- **Blocked members are no longer told.** Sending to someone who blocked you used to show "the user has blocked you" + a "Blocked (No actions available)" menu. Now WhatsApp behaviour: the message is accepted, shown with a **single grey tick forever**, never delivered — the server stores it `hidden_from_receiver` and skips notification/push/WS; the blocker's history, chat-list preview, unread count and mark-as-read all exclude it. `getConversationData(conversationId, viewerId)` now sends the viewer id (derived synchronously on the first load — `decryptedUserId` is still '' then). The sender-blocked-them case is unchanged ("Please unblock…"). Presence (online/last seen) stays hidden from the blocked member.
- **Free members only learn that a message arrived.** A member without the MESSAGE entitlement already hits the upgrade wall on opening a chat, but the **chat-list preview printed the full last message** — now masked server-side to "🔒 New message — upgrade to read" (system lines and their own sent messages stay), and the in-app notification row no longer embeds the text ("sent you a message. Upgrade to read it."). Push already said "Someone sent you a message".
tsc 56 unchanged. Rides the next APK + backend jar (ddl-auto adds `chats.hidden_from_receiver`).

The card already existed in `InlineProfileTabs` but was wrapped in `interests.length > 0` and sat below the Contact card — and **most live profiles have no hobbies saved** (probed the live API: only 701/707 in the 700–756 range do), so the section looked absent. (2) `SectionCard` cast a 12px-radius shadow offset 4px but sat only **8px** apart, so each shadow spilled onto the next card; now `marginBottom: 14`, a softer 8px/2px shadow, `elevation` 3→2 and the app's `#e7edf5` card hairline. 💡 Keep card spacing larger than the shadow's reach (radius + y-offset).

- **🔴 A 9-digit mobile registered fine** (production user 925 = `987456321`) — the only length check was the blur hint. New `formatHeight`/`formatWeight` (legacy values: `5'6"`, `5.5"`, `3.0`, `"65 kg"`, `50`, NULL). **Empty fields now ALWAYS render as `-`** (client ask) — `DetailField`'s `showEmpty` default flipped to `true`, and emptiness uses `isBlankValue` so a real `0` is not shown as `-`. Hero subline no longer leaves a dangling `, •`. `SearchResult` stopped appending `"` blindly (was `5'6""`).

Client: editing Date of Birth on the profile page allowed under-18 dates, unlike signup. **Regression** — the retired `editProfileModal.tsx` had the check (`age < 18` → "Age Restriction" warning), but `EditProfileSection.tsx` only set `maximumDate={new Date()}`. The backend (`UserDetailService.updatePersonalInfo`) already rejects under-18 with a 400, so the member found out only after pressing Save ("Could not update"). Now: both pickers cap at `latestAllowedDob()` (today − 18y), a `pickDob()` guard shows the same future-date / "Age Restriction" warnings as sign-up, and `validate()` blocks saving a stored under-18 value (legacy data).
