### 2026-09-15 — Cashfree online checkout behind `PAYMENT_MODE=GATEWAY` (cross-repo)
Backend now has a Cashfree integration (see the backend CLAUDE.md 2026-09-15 entry + `CASHFREE_INTEGRATION_PLAN.md`). App side:
- **`PaymentMode` gains `'GATEWAY'`** in `utils/upgradeNavigation.ts`; `PaymentScreen.tsx` now IMPORTS that type (`CatalogPaymentMode | 'LOADING'`) instead of redeclaring its own union — the drift this caused before is gone. `AddOnPaymentScreen`'s local union also accepts it. Routing: GATEWAY behaves like QR (Free users → `PremiumTab` → `PaymentScreen`).
- **New `components/GatewayCheckoutView.tsx`** — plan summary + "Pay ₹X securely". `userApi.createCashfreeOrder(encodedUserId, {planId, purpose})` (amount decided server-side) → `WebBrowser.openAuthSessionAsync('https://vaibhavvivaahamatrimony.com/pay/<orderId>', 'vaibhavvivaaha://screens/PaymentReturn')` → `router.replace('/(root)/screens/PaymentReturn', {order_id})`. **System browser on purpose, not `react-native-webview`**: UPI intent apps (GPay/PhonePe) need a real browser hand-off. Rendered by `PaymentScreen` (purpose SUBSCRIPTION) and `AddOnPaymentScreen` (BOOST_PURCHASE, `planId=null`).
- **New `screens/PaymentReturn.tsx`** (HEADERLESS in `screens/_layout.tsx`) — polls `userApi.getCashfreeOrderStatus(orderId, encodedUserId)` every 2.5 s for ~40 s; states paid / failed (retry re-opens `/pay`) / expired / pending / error. Reached by the replace above AND by the website's return page deep link `vaibhavvivaaha://screens/PaymentReturn?order_id=…` (same `screens/<Name>` convention as the profile deep link). On Android both fire; the replace is idempotent.
- Dead `userApi.createOrder` (Razorpay) replaced by `createCashfreeOrder` / `getCashfreeOrderStatus`.
- ⚠ **Do NOT flip `PAYMENT_MODE` to `GATEWAY` in production yet.** Opening an online checkout for membership from inside the app is alternative billing for digital content under Google Play policy — the whole reason CONTACT/INFO exist. Needs the Play alternative-billing (India) enrolment decision + a privacy-policy update (it currently says no third-party payment gateway) first. The website flow has no such constraint.
- tsc: 56 (baseline, unchanged). Not device-tested yet — needs backend deploy + Cashfree sandbox keys.

### 2026-09-09 (later) — Edit page: whitespace rule on every input, unsaved-changes guard, Save clear of the nav bar
Three follow-ups on the new full-page section editor:
- **Leading/trailing space blocked on ALL inputs**, not just names. New `textInput()` refuses a leading space as it is typed and collapses repeated spaces (multi-line fields keep their newlines); the trailing one is trimmed **on blur and again in the payload** — it cannot be blocked while typing, since you must be able to type a space to start the next word, and a member can hit Save straight from the keyboard without ever blurring.
- **Leaving with unsaved edits now asks first.** `isDirty` diffs the trimmed form against a trimmed baseline of the values the page was opened with, and both exits go through `attemptLeave()` → Discard changes / Keep editing. ⚠️ The **Android hardware back** needed its own `BackHandler` interception (returning `true` to hold the screen) — the back pill's prompt alone let hardware back walk straight out. ⚠️ The "already saved" flag is **state, not a ref**: as a ref it never retriggered the `isDirty` memo, so pressing hardware back while the success popup was up still prompted about unsaved changes.
- **Save button sat under the Android navigation bar.** The `SafeAreaView` excludes the bottom edge on purpose (so the scroll runs to the screen edge), which leaves the footer row to reserve that strip itself — it was using a flat `Platform`-based padding. Now `Math.max(insets.bottom, 10) + 8`.

tsc 56 unchanged. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-09 — Profile editing rebuilt as full pages; registration counts toward completion; 19-item client batch
Large client batch. The centrepiece: **`components/editProfileModal.tsx` is retired** (deprecation header left on the file — it is still imported by the dead `components/tabs.tsx` → `profile_backup.tsx` chain) and replaced by **`app/(root)/screens/EditProfileSection.tsx`**, a headerless route pushed per section. Three structural reasons a restyle could not fix it:
- its Save button sat under the keyboard, and focusing Current Address opened-then-closed the IME (a text input at the bottom of a bottom sheet has no scroll range — same class as the Help & Support textarea);
- it drew its own control system (`SelectList`, grey boxes) unrelated to the register form;
- 🔴 **its dropdowns were HARDCODED arrays that had drifted from the keyValue master lists** — the recurring vocabulary-mismatch bug, sixth instance. Its `doshams` offered `"None"`/`"Manglik (Chevvai Dosham)"` and `stars` offered `Kiruthigai`/`Punarpoosam`/`Mirugasritam` — the pre-normalisation spellings the 2026-08-17 data migration removed. **Editing your profile could write a dosham/star the search dropdown no longer offers, silently making you unfindable.** The new page reads the same `education` / `employingIn` / `annualIncomeRegister` / `star` / `dosham` keyValue rows the register form uses, guarded on those SPECIFIC keys (never "is masterData non-empty" — §17).

The new page also carries the rest of the batch: **every registration field is mandatory** (First/Last Name, DOB, Education, Occupation, Employing In) with inline errors; **char caps on every input** (names 50, occupation 60, address/education-in-detail 200 with a live counter, job place 100, parents' names/occupations 50, sibling counts 2); **Occupation is free text**, matching registration, instead of a 70-entry dropdown that could not represent what the member typed at signup; **Subcaste, Caste, Religion and Gender are readonly** — ⚠️ Gender because `update-personal-info` has NO gender field, so the old editable control silently discarded the change. **`No of Siblings` is now derived** from brothers+sisters rather than a third dropdown that could disagree with them, and **married counts are validated against their totals** ("You have 1 sisters — married cannot be more").

**🔴 Fresh registrants were told their profile was 0% complete.** `UserDetailService.calculateProfileCompletion`'s `requiredFields` listed ONLY the 26 post-signup extras, so someone who had just filled the entire registration form scored 0/26. Verified live on user 923: 0%, with all 26 listed as missing. The 9 registration-collected fields (firstName, lastName, dateOfBirth, gender, caste, education, occupation, employedAt, location) now count — 9/35 ≈ **25% straight after signup**.

**🔴 Profile % went stale after an edit.** The profile tab's 30s `CACHE_MS` throttle also swallowed genuine changes: delete the horoscope → still 100%, visit Home → 96%, come back → 96% (exact client repro). New `utils/profileRefresh.ts` one-shot dirty flag lets a real edit beat the throttle, and the stats/completion fetch was extracted from its focus effect into `refreshStats()` so horoscope + gallery mutations refresh the number immediately.

**Register page (client: "1st page 2nd page overlapping while keyboard opens… too much space at the bottom")** — the spacer padded the FULL keyboard height, but the Next/Back row sits OUTSIDE the scroll view and the keyboard covers it first, so ~90px of that padding was pure dead band; now `keyboardHeight - navRowHeight`, with navRowHeight measured via the row's own `onLayout`. The Swiper is wrapped in `overflow: 'hidden'` — it sizes its pages at mount, so a keyboard-driven height change let them bleed into each other.

**Also:** login welcome popup used only the first name (the header greeting already showed both); names reject a leading space, collapse repeated spaces, cap at 50 and trim on blur AND at submit (client: no front/back space, spaces allowed in between); the "Vaibhav Vivaha · v2.1" footer block is gone from the profile page; `annualIncomeRegister` extended live in production from 30 lakhs to **10-lakh steps up to 100 lakhs + "Above 1 Crore"** (23 entries, keyValue only — no rebuild).

tsc 56 unchanged throughout. App changes ride the next APK; the completion fix rides the backend deploy.

### 2026-09-05 (later 6) — "Premium Feature / Upgrade Now" gate restyled onto the empty-state system; dead premium wall deleted from mailBox
Client: a Free member tapping Home → **Likes** (and **Views**) gets the Premium Feature / Upgrade Now screen — put it on the same theme as the empty states. Same screen (`ListUser.tsx`), so this covers **Likes, Views and Matches** in one change (all three are `ListUser` types: `whoLikedMe` / `viewed` / `connection`).

**The gate now literally REUSES the empty state's styles** — `emptyIconRing` / `emptyIconCircle` / `emptyTitle` / `emptySubtitle` / `emptyCta` / `emptyCtaInner` / `emptyCtaText` — rather than declaring a parallel set. Blue ring (`ACCENT_BLUE` at 9% via `withAlpha`), white disc, ink title, slate subtitle, `#1F7FE5 → #1862b8` CTA pill: identical to the Explore → Saved Search empty state. Only the glyph differs (a crown, since this is a locked feature rather than an empty list). Sharing the styles instead of copying the values is what stops the two from drifting apart again.

⚠️ **The parallel `upgrade*` style set that used to live here is deleted** — it had drifted into its own palette (cream `#fef3c7 → #fde68a` disc, dark-gold crown, **maroon `#9c4040` CTA**) and its own type scale (`Rubik-ExtraBold` 20 — a face reserved app-wide for the brand wordmark in `VVMWelcomeHeader`). 💡 A first pass at this restyled the gate in the app's **gold premium language** (`#F6B733 → #C59A40`, matching `CommonPopup`'s premium medallion) on the reasoning that gold means "paid" everywhere else. The client rejected it: they want **one** centre-state theme across the app, not a per-semantic palette. **Centre states are blue here regardless of whether they are empty, errored or plan-gated** — the crown glyph and the copy carry the "premium" meaning, not the colour.

**The error state on the same screen was aligned too** (it sits beside the other two and still had legacy maroon): type scale matched to the others, and the "Try again" button moved from maroon to the app blue — retrying is a neutral action, so it must borrow neither the gold that means "paid" nor the red that means "failed". **Left alone deliberately:** the per-row avatar rings and the count badge still use `meta.gradient`/maroon — that is the list's card identity, a bigger design decision than was asked for.

**Home → Proposals needed no gate restyle: it has no gate.** Checked, and found `mailBox.tsx` carried a dead `PremiumRequiredScreen` (pink `#ec4899` "Premium Required" wall) plus a `LoadingScreen` and an `isPremium` state — the state was computed by an effect and **never read**, and neither component was ever rendered. Unwired scaffolding that made the tab look plan-gated to anyone reading the file. Deleted, along with its 9 orphaned styles and the now-unused `Pressable` import and `useSubscription` call. tsc 56 unchanged, both files clean. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-05 (later 5) — 🔴 Help & Support keyboard opened a sliver then closed itself — the *fix attempt* was causing it
Client: tap the "Your message" textarea, the keyboard rises a little from the bottom and immediately dismisses. An earlier uncommitted attempt on this screen had added a measured keyboard spacer (the chatscreen/sign-up technique) and it did not help — **because that spacer was the cause**.

**Why it dismissed the IME.** The spacer drove `contentContainerStyle`'s `paddingBottom`, so on focus the scroll content's height swung **0 → keyboardHeight → 0** inside the IME animation window: `keyboardDidShow` fires and adds a full keyboard height of padding, then the container-shrank measurement lands a frame later and cancels it again. Resizing the scroll content by ~300px twice while Android is animating the IME, with `keyboardOpeningTime={0}` telling KeyboardAwareScrollView to auto-scroll at the same moment, makes Android give up and dismiss. Removed entirely — `contentContainerStyle` is now a **static** style, which cannot oscillate.

⚠️ **The spacer's stated premise was factually wrong**, which is worth remembering before reaching for that technique again: its comment claimed "the message textarea is the LAST field in this scroll", but the **Quick help section always renders below it** (`FALLBACK_SUPPORT.quickHelp` ships 3 items, so it renders even with no keyValue row). The scroll range sign-up had to synthesise already existed here. `scrollContent.paddingBottom` 48 → 120 as a static safety margin for the hypothetical case of a `SUPPORT_CONTENT` row with an empty `quickHelp`.

**Its measurement was also racy on Android** even on its own terms: `keyboardDidShow` fires *after* the window resize, so the `keyboardHeight === 0 && containerHeight > 0` effect could capture the ALREADY-SHRUNKEN height as the "keyboard closed" baseline, permanently poisoning `containerShrank`.

💡 **The distinction that matters: sign-up measures because its last field genuinely is last and its CTA sits outside the scroll. Do not copy that machinery onto a screen that already has content below the input — there it is not just unnecessary, it actively breaks focus.** The two earlier landmine entries (chatscreen 2026-08-13, sign-up 2026-08-23) say "measure, don't assume"; this one adds "and first check whether you need to measure at all".

**Kept from the earlier attempt** (both correct): module-level `HELP_SCREEN_OPTIONS` so `<Stack.Screen options>` is not a fresh object every render, and `KeyboardAwareScrollView` instead of a plain `ScrollView`. **Cleaned up:** the abandoned attempt also left `Keyboard`/`Modal`/`KeyboardAvoidingView`/`ScrollView`/`useRef`/`useEffect` imported but unused and four orphan `modal*` styles from a "put the form in a Modal" experiment — no Modal is rendered on this screen. tsc 56 unchanged, file clean. Rides the next APK.

**Same screen, back arrow not rendering.** The in-page back pill drew a **`ChevronRight` flipped with `style={{transform:[{rotate:'180deg'}]}}`**. A lucide icon is a `react-native-svg` `<Svg>`, and react-native-svg reads `transform` with **SVG** semantics rather than React Native's array-of-operations form, so the glyph collapsed instead of rotating. Now a plain `ChevronLeft`, no transform — matching sibling `FAQPage`, which draws its back pill with a plain `ArrowLeft`. Both copies (main render **and** the post-submit success state) were fixed; `ChevronRight` stays imported because the channel rows still use it untransformed, which is why *those* chevrons always rendered.

💡 **For a fixed direction, import the icon that already points that way — don't rotate one.** ⚠️ Deliberately NOT changed: `SearchTabs.tsx`'s `chevronIcon`/`chevronRotated` accordion pair uses the same rotate trick, but there the rotation is a **toggle** between collapsed and expanded, which no static icon can express; a transform that silently no-ops there leaves the chevron visible and merely un-flipped, not invisible.

### 2026-09-05 (later 4) — 🔴 Explore "Clear" left both range sliders where they were dragged; income filter was applied even untouched
Client: drag Age or Annual Income, tap **Clear**, the thumbs stay put. Confirmed, plus two related defects found while auditing every field.

**🔴 The sliders are driven by their OWN state, not by `filters`.** `handleClearFilters` reset the `filters` object (all 10 keys — it matches the initial state exactly, verified) and the two toggles, but never `fromAge`/`toAge`/`fromIncome`/`toIncome`. So after Clear the thumbs and the Minimum/Maximum boxes still showed the dragged values **while the search fell back to 18–60** — the form said one thing and searched another. Now reset alongside. `rn-range-slider` IS properly controlled (`useLowHigh` prefers the prop, and an effect calls `updateThumbs()` when `lowProp`/`highProp` change), so moving the state is all that's needed — the library was never the problem.

**🔴 An untouched income slider still filtered.** `RangeSlider.updateThumbs()` calls `onValueChanged(low, high, false)` **on mount** and again on every prop change — so `filters.annualIncomeFilter` was populated as `"0 Lakhs - 99 Lakhs"` before the member touched anything, and re-populated right after Clear resets the thumbs. `gatherSearchData` turned that into a real `minAnnualIncome/maxAnnualIncome` pair, and **`NULL BETWEEN 0 AND 9900000` is NULL, not true** — so every profile with no recorded income was silently dropped from *every* search, cleared or not. Now a full-span slider sends `null` (no filter). Age needed no equivalent change: its `''` fallback and a full-span `"18 Yrs - 60 Yrs"` both parse to `['18','60']`.

**🔴 Loading a saved search moved the income slider but never the age slider** — the mirror of the Clear bug. The `age`/`agerange` case set only `updatedFilters.ageRange`, so the thumbs kept the *previous* search's position while the text said something else. Both sliders are now applied in one pass after the loop, seeded to full span so a saved search that carries no age/income filter **resets** them instead of inheriting whatever was on screen. Both parses are guarded with `Number.isFinite` + clamp, so a malformed stored value can't push `NaN` into a slider (which would render at min and then report `NaN` straight back into `filters`).

⚠️ **`toIncome` was seeded at `100` while its slider's `max` is `99`.** The slider clamps internally but the React state stayed 100, and the Maximum label branches on `toIncome === 99` — so it read "₹100L" on first paint and "₹100L+" after any interaction. New module-level `AGE_MIN/AGE_MAX/INCOME_MIN/INCOME_MAX` constants now feed the state seeds, both `<RangeSlider>`s, both `RangeSelectorModal`s, the label branches and the Clear reset, so those bounds can't drift apart again.

**Audited every other control on the criteria tab** — subcaste / education / star / dosham / jobSector chips, city, profileCreatedBy, degree, and the Photos-only / Horoscope-only switches all write to `filters` and were already reset correctly. `profileId` belongs to the By Profile ID tab (Clear only renders on `criteria`) and `expandedSections` is accordion UI, both deliberately untouched. 💡 `minAgeText`/`maxAgeText`/`minSalaryText`/`maxSalaryText`/`selectedEducation`/`selectedCity`/`value` are **dead state feeding a dead `handleFindPress`** (never called; their setters have zero call sites) left over from the removed FindPartner grid — they cannot diverge from their defaults, so Clear does not need them. tsc 56 unchanged, SearchTabs 15 pre-existing unchanged. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-05 (later 3) — 🔴 Request-tab empty states never centred, and "Sent By You" showed an ERROR instead of one
Client: all three Request sub-tabs show a bare "No Interests Found" when empty; make it a proper centred empty state with an Explore Matches button. Two real bugs behind the look:

**🔴 `styles.emptyState` sets `flex: 1` but `listContent` had no `flexGrow: 1`.** A FlatList's `ListEmptyComponent` only gets height to flex into when the **contentContainerStyle** grows — otherwise the container collapses to its content (here just `paddingBottom`), so the message rendered jammed near the top in a ~120px box. That is the "not on page center" report. `flexGrow: 1` added (harmless with rows — it only grows when content is shorter than the frame), plus `style={{flex:1}}` on the three lists so the list itself is bounded rather than content-sized.

⚠️ **The 90px spacer `<View>` after ReceivedTab's list was removed in the same change** — it only existed because the unbounded list overflowed the screen and pushed it out of sight. Once the list takes `flex:1` that spacer becomes a visible dead band above the footer, and `contentContainerStyle`'s `footerPad` already provides the real clearance.

**🔴 "Sent By You" could never reach its empty state at all** — `loadSentData` treated any non-200 as a failure (`setError('No Interest Sent Record Found')`) and the component early-returns on `error`. But `getSentMailbox` answers a member with nothing sent as **`code: 404` / `data: null`**, the same HTTP-200-with-a-code-in-the-body pattern that crashed the home shelves on 2026-08-30. So a normal new member got an error screen, not an empty state. Now `200 || 404` → coerce to `[]`; only a genuine failure sets `error`.

**New shared `MailboxEmptyState`** (icon ring + ink title + slate subtitle + blue gradient "Explore Matches" pill → `/(root)/(tabs)/explore`, the same target `myChatList.tsx` already uses) replaces all 4 empty states and all 4 error states. Copy is now per-context instead of "No Interests Found" everywhere: Received branches on the selected filter (pending/accepted/declined — "no interests found" under the Declined chip reads as though the whole inbox is empty), Sent says nothing sent yet, and the two Permissions lists talk about **access requests**, not interests — that tab was never about interests.

⚠️ **This screen has NO `RefreshControl` anywhere** (the tabs refetch via `useFocusEffect`), so error copy must not say "pull down to refresh" — it instructs a gesture that does nothing. Error strings also stopped concatenating raw `err.message` into member-facing text, now that they render as a prominent subtitle rather than one grey line. tsc 56 unchanged. Rides the next APK.

**Same pass on Explore → Saved Search** (`SearchTabs.tsx`, the *real* Explore tabs — `components/ExploreTabs.tsx` is still dead code, see §8.4). Identical `flexGrow: 1` fix on that tab's ScrollView `contentContainerStyle` plus `flex: 1` on `emptyStateContainer` (its `marginTop: 20` is gone — that biases a centred block downward), and the same ring/disc icon + ink title + slate subtitle treatment so the two screens read as one system. Its CTA is **"Start a New Search" → `setActiveTab('criteria')`**, not Explore Matches: a saved search is created by running a search from this very screen, so sending the member to the Explore feed would walk them away from the thing they're missing. `exploreEmptyIcon` was orphaned by the swap and deleted. That tab's fetch was already safe (only sets state on `code === 200`, never sets an `error`), so it had no equivalent of the SentTab bug. `emptyStateContainer`/`emptyStateText`/`emptyStateSubtext` are used ONLY by this empty state — verified before restyling them.

**Same pass on `ListUser.tsx`** — the destination for Home's **Likes** (`whoLikedMe`), **Matched** (`connection`) and **Views** (`viewed`) stat cells, plus Shortlisted / Who Shortlisted You / Contact Reveals, so all 6 list types are covered at once. This screen needed **no centring fix** — its `centerState` already has `flex: 1` and is a direct branch of the SafeAreaView, not a `ListEmptyComponent` inside a scroll view — and its fetch already treats `code === 404` as "empty, not an error", so it had neither of the two bugs found on the other screens. What it lacked was an action and a decent icon: the glyph was flat `#d1d5db` on `#f3f4f6`, grey-on-grey, which reads as a **disabled** state rather than an empty one. Now the same ring/disc + ink title + slate subtitle + "Explore Matches" pill.

⚠️ **The ring takes the tab's own `meta.accent`** (pink for Likes, maroon for Views/Connections, blue for Reveals) so each list stays identifiable, **while the CTA stays the app blue** — same action, same treatment everywhere. New `withAlpha(hex, a)` helper builds the ring's tint: the accents are plain 6-digit hex and RN only accepts 8-digit `#RRGGBBAA` as a literal, so it emits `rgba()` instead. Falls back to the app blue on an unparseable value, so adding a TAB_META entry with a named colour degrades instead of crashing.

💡 **Rule worth keeping: `flex: 1` on a ListEmptyComponent (or any empty block inside a scroll view) does nothing unless the CONTENT CONTAINER sets `flexGrow: 1`.** Two of these three screens had the `flex: 1` and looked like a pure styling miss; the centring was one property away the whole time.

### 2026-09-05 (later 13) — 🔴 Registration: "failed" but account created in background; SAME EMAIL could register N accounts
Client repro confirmed IN PRODUCTION: `hackerbalakumar@gmail.com` on users 913 AND 914 (mobiles …9999/…9958) — plus legacy dupes (one email ×30, ×12, ×6). Three defects, three fixes:
- **`createUser` checked ONLY mobile uniqueness, never email.** The OTP step does block registered emails — but a retry reuses the already-verified email session and never re-runs that check, so mobile-changed retries minted new accounts against the same email. Now a second gate inside createUser: registered email → **409 "This email is already registered. Please sign in instead."**
- **`createUser` was NOT transactional** — the user row saves early, heavy JSON-building follows; a late exception (or the client giving up) left "failed" + a ghost account. Now `@Transactional` **with an explicit `setRollbackOnly()` in the catch** — load-bearing because the method swallows its own exceptions (HTTP-200 pattern), so without the manual mark the early save would still COMMIT. ⚠️ Any HTTP-200-pattern method that writes then catches needs this same mark if made transactional.
- **App:** createUser gets `timeout: 30000` (the global 10s killed slow-network signups mid-flight — the other source of "failed but created"), 401/409 both show "Already Registered → please sign in", and a TIMEOUT error now says the account may still have been created and to try SIGNING IN first — retry-blind copy was manufacturing the duplicates.
`findByEmail` was already duplicate-safe (newest-first default method), so email login survives the existing dupes. ⚠️ **Data cleanup pending, needs client confirmation**: users 913/914 (their test) and the legacy 901–906 junk-mobile batch should be deduped, THEN a `UNIQUE(email)` index becomes possible (multiple NULLs are fine in MySQL). Backend compile clean; jar rebuilding; app tsc 56.

### 2026-09-05 (later 12) — Upgrade taps stacked N screens; settings rows debounced + loader
Client: on settings, "Upgrade to Premium" could be tapped N times → N plan screens pushed; clicks generally slow, app sometimes "stuck". Root cause: **`buildUpgradeAction`'s Free-user path `await`ed `resolvePaymentMode()` (a NETWORK call, up to 10s in a stall window) before `router.push`, with zero feedback** — every queued tap eventually pushed. Fixed at the source so all ~25 upsell call sites inherit it: module-level single-flight guard in `buildUpgradeAction` (busy flag + 1.2s post-navigation lock), and `resolvePaymentModeFast()` races the fresh fetch against a 700ms cached-value fallback — a tap always navigates promptly, fresh mode still wins on a healthy network (the 2026-07-20 fetch-fresh correctness holds in the normal case). settingsPage: shared `go()` 800ms tap-debounce on ALL 15 navigation rows (double-tap = two stacked copies was the "stuck" feel), and the Upgrade Now row shows an `ActivityIndicator` in place of its Premium badge while the action resolves. tsc 56. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-05 (later 11) — 🔴 Slow/failing image uploads: profile-edit tabs uploaded the RAW camera original; "Birth Chart" → "Horoscope"
Client: bigger images take ~2 minutes and sometimes fail (profile photo, gallery, horoscope). Root cause: **`components/tabs.tsx`'s three upload handlers (photo add, horoscope add, horoscope update) never got August's compression fix** — they uploaded the raw 5–12MB `quality: 1` original with the old URI-sniffed MIME type (the exact bug fixed in profile.tsx on 2026-08-13). A 5–12MB body on a ~1Mbps mobile upstream IS minutes, and blows timeouts/nginx caps. All three now use `prepareImageForUpload` (1600px/q0.7 → ~150–400KB, seconds to upload) + reject unsupported types; dead `imageUri/mimeType/fileName` debug logs removed. Also confirmed every multipart method in userApi carries an explicit upload timeout (60–120s; `replaceGalleryImage` was missing one — the global 10s would have killed it). 💡 When one screen's upload pipeline gets fixed, grep for the OTHER copies — tabs.tsx duplicates profile.tsx's media flows and missed two fixes now. **Profile "Birth Chart" section renamed "Horoscope"** (client wording). tsc 56. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-05 (later 10) — Rejection-email "open app" button now opens the APP (deep-link chain, cross-repo)
The rejection email's button went to the website: backend `app.link` (the `{{appLink}}` merge var in every ZeptoMail template) was literally `https://vaibhavvivaahamatrimony.com/`. Email clients (Gmail especially) block custom-scheme links, so the fix is the standard https-landing pattern:
- **Web: new `/open-app` page** — immediately tries `vaibhavvivaaha://` (opens the installed app on Android AND iOS — a rejected member then lands on ProfileUnderVerificationScreen automatically via the root redirect), and if still visible after ~1.6s falls back to the **Play Store** on Android / website on iOS (swap in the App Store URL when iOS ships). Manual "Open the App" / "Get it on Google Play" buttons stay visible for in-app browsers that block auto-navigation.
- **Web: `public/.well-known/assetlinks.json`** now hosted (package `com.vaibhavvivaaha.app` + the UPLOAD key's SHA-256) — the missing half of the 2026-08-12 App Links work. ⚠️ **Play App Signing re-signs the app: the "App signing key certificate" SHA-256 from Play Console → App integrity must be ADDED to this file** or Android link verification will fail; the upload-key entry alone is not enough.
- **Backend: `app.link` → `https://vaibhavvivaahamatrimony.com/open-app`** (affects approval + rejection emails alike — both should open the app).
⚠️ **Verify the ZeptoMail rejection template's button href is `{{appLink}}`** in the Zoho console — if the URL was hardcoded in the template, it must be edited there; code can only change the merge value. Web dist rebuilt; jar rebuilt (16:0x) — deploy web + jar.

Now any unknown-but-real plan title renders AS ITSELF (blue treatment); only an empty/free title is Free. Future campaign tiers inherit this automatically.

### 2026-09-05 (later 8) — Help&Support keyboard fix v5, INLINE as the client requires: plain ScrollView was the culprit pattern
Client rejected the Modal composer (v4 — keyboard reportedly still didn't open there either) and requires the textarea inline on the page. The correlation that finally holds across every screen: **keyboard works on Change PIN (NO scroll container), sign-up (KeyboardAwareScrollView) and chat (input outside the list); it failed ONLY on this screen — the one keyboard screen with a plain RN ScrollView.** Fixed by swapping the ScrollView for `KeyboardAwareScrollView` with sign-up's exact proven props (`enableOnAndroid`, `enableAutomaticScroll`, `extraScrollHeight` 45 Android/30 iOS, `keyboardOpeningTime={0}`, persistTaps handled, `enableResetScrollToCoords={false}`), restored the inline chips+textarea+send form, and deleted the Modal composer entirely (inline `formError` text kept in place of popups). Measured `kbSpacer` retained in contentContainer padding (sign-up runs the same combination). 💡 If an in-page input ever refuses the keyboard again: check the scroll container FIRST — plain ScrollView is the outlier pattern on this device. tsc 56. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-05 (later 7) — Help&Support message form moved into a Modal (keyboard fix v4 — the working one by construction)
Three in-page fixes (measured spacer, identity-stable header options, going headerless) were each VERIFIED APPLIED via the `[help-kb]` device log and the keyboard still closed itself the instant it opened. Stopped fighting it: **the composer is now an RN Modal** — a fake-input trigger card on the page opens "Send us a message" (category chips + autoFocus textarea + send) in its own native window, where keyboards demonstrably hold on this device (delete-account modal, edit sheets, SupportFAB chat all work). Submit unchanged (`createSupportTicket`, code===200 checked). ⚠️ Errors inside the composer render INLINE (`formError` text), never `popup.*` — that would stack a second native Modal over this one, the exact freeze class fixed same-day on the gallery upload. The in-page cause remains unidentified (suspect device/IME-specific behaviour on scrollable non-modal surfaces); if other in-page inputs ever blink, reach for a Modal first. tsc 56. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-05 (later 6) — Login accepts email OR mobile; Settings Change PIN requires the CURRENT pin
- **Email-or-mobile login** (cross-repo): LoginScreen's identifier field now takes either — label "Email or Mobile Number", `keyboardType="email-address"`, pure digits keep the 10-digit mobile behaviour while anything else is treated as an email (validated `email-regex OR ^\d{10}$`). Payload still sends the identifier in `mobile`; the backend routes by shape (`contains('@')` → `findByEmail`, else `findByMobile` with the family-login fall-through kept mobile-only — family PINs are provisioned against a mobile). ⚠️ **The 'mobileNumber' AsyncStorage key now stores `response.data.data.mobile`, NOT the typed input** — an email login would otherwise poison the key Change PIN and the self profile read. MySQL's ci collation makes the email lookup case-insensitive for free.
- **Settings → Change PIN gained a "Current PIN" field** (settings flow ONLY — forgot-PIN/reset flows have no old pin and are untouched): new backend `POST /user/changePinWithOld` `{mobileNumber, oldPin, newPin}` verifies the stored Base64 pin and returns **401 "Current PIN is incorrect"** on mismatch; the screen shows a specific "Incorrect PIN" alert for that code. `userApi.changePinWithOld`. The old `/user/changePin` endpoint is unchanged for the reset flows.

Backend compile clean; app tsc 56. Both need the backend deploy (fresh `target/app.jar`) + APK.

### 2026-09-05 (later 5) — deleted_accounts tombstone table + exit reason; Help&Support keyboard fix v3 (headerless)
- **Delete account now records WHO left and WHY** (cross-repo): new backend `deleted_accounts` table (snapshot: userId, memberId, name, gender, mobile, email, casteId, reason ≤500; `createdAt` = deletion time; NOT in the wipe list — it exists to survive the member). `UserService.deleteAccount(encodedUserId, reason)` captures the snapshot BEFORE the wipe and saves it only AFTER the wipe succeeds — a row means the deletion really happened; a failed tombstone insert never fails the deletion. Controller takes optional `?reason=`. App: the delete modal gained an optional "Why are you leaving?" multiline (250 cap) above the typed-DELETE confirm; `userApi.deleteAccount(id, reason)` passes it; cleared on modal open. ddl-auto creates the table on deploy.
- **Help&Support keyboard, attempt 3**: stable header options (v2) did NOT fix it on device. Decisive structural move: **the screen is now HEADERLESS** (registered in screens/_layout.tsx) with an in-page back pill + title like FAQPage — every keyboard screen that works (chat, sign-up, FAQ search) is headerless; this was the only one with a native header. Measured spacer kept. If v3 also fails on device, the next suspect list is empty — escalate to testing on a second device / expo-doctor.

Backend compile clean, app tsc 56. Needs backend deploy (same jar queue) + APK/dev reload.

### 2026-09-05 (later 4) — Explore filters: "Job & Education Details" label; ALL locked filters now use the standard upsell popup; Help&Support keyboard root-caused to header options identity
- **SearchTabs**: section header "Job Details" → "Job & Education Details".
- **The Explore form's private "Upgrade to Premium" bottom modal is DELETED** — its Upgrade Now button was a dead end (`router.push('/premium')` commented out; it just closed). Every gated control now calls the shared centred `popup.premiumRequired` via a new `lockedFilterUpsell(featureText, featureName, minPlan)` helper: Subcaste (Silver floor), Education dropdown+pill, City, Star, Dosham, horoscope-only switch (Classic floor — displays "Silver" while Classic is retired, via `upgradeMessage`). ⚠️ **The string `onPress={() => setShowUpgradeModal(true)}` existed in COMMENTED-OUT JSX blocks too** — an occurrence-order replacement landed two labels in dead comments and mislabeled the live Education pill as Star; caught by the leftover `setShowUpgradeModal` compile errors. When batch-replacing in this file, check comments first.
- **HelpSupportPage keyboard blink — real cause found** (the measured spacer was applied and it STILL closed, per `[help-kb]` device log): this is the only keyboard screen with a native stack header, and `<Stack.Screen options={{title}}/>` was an inline literal in BOTH return branches — a fresh object every render, so the focus-ring re-render pushed `navigation.setOptions` the instant the keyboard opened, and the native header update blurred the input. Options hoisted to a module-level constant. 💡 Rule: **on screens with a native header and text inputs, Stack.Screen options must be identity-stable.**

tsc 56 unchanged. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-05 (later 3) — FAQ "Help Centre" pill removed; Help & Support keyboard self-closing fixed
- **FAQPage**: the header's "Help Centre" pill was a decorative `View` styled like a button — looked tappable, went nowhere. Removed (back pill stays).
- **HelpSupportPage**: tapping "Your message" opened the keyboard which immediately closed itself. The textarea is the LAST field in the scroll with fixed bottom padding — no scroll range to lift it — and some Android IMEs close when the focused editor cannot be brought into view. This was also the ONLY screen still on a `KeyboardAvoidingView` with `behavior: undefined` (a no-op on Android). Converted to the proven measured pattern (chatscreen 2026-08-13 / sign-up 2026-08-23): `SafeAreaView onLayout` baseline, >100px shrink guard, spacer = 0 if the OS resized else full keyboard height, appended to the scroll content's paddingBottom; `[help-kb]` log matches the other two. tsc 56 unchanged. ### 2026-09-05 — PAYMENT_MODE flipped INFO → CONTACT in production (post-approval)
The app is approved/published, so prices are back: client reported plan amounts "flash for a second then disappear" on UpgradePlanScreen — that WAS INFO mode (optimistic paint with prices, then the mode fetch strips them). Flipped the keyValue row to `{"mode": "CONTACT"}` (verified live). Amounts now show on the plan picker AND on the RM/features view after selecting; payment remains the contact-RM flow, no QR. ⚠️ **Operational rule: flip back to INFO before submitting ANY app update for Play review, and back to CONTACT after approval** — updates are re-reviewed against whatever the server serves that day. One-line flip, 5-min cache TTL, no rebuild either way.

### 2026-09-05 (later 2) — Replace/Remove photo: 400ms beat after popup.hide() before picker/confirm; nav animation 300→220ms
"Replace with a new photo" never opened the camera roll: the sheet ran `popup.hide()` and launched the system picker in the same tick — Android silently refuses to present a picker over a still-dismissing native Modal. 400ms beat added between hide and launch; same beat on "Remove photo" (its confirm re-opens the SAME CommonPopup Modal — hide→show in one tick can leave it never appearing). Last two raw `res.data.message` leaks (gallery delete, horoscope delete) genericized. 💡 Companion rule to the stacked-Modal freeze: **never present a native surface while another Modal is mid-dismiss.** Stack `animationDuration` 300→220 for snappier navigation feel. tsc 56 unchanged.

### 2026-09-05 — 🔴 Screen froze after a successful gallery upload: two stacked native Modals
Client repro: upload a gallery photo → success, image appears → **nothing on screen responds**. Cause: the upload overlay was a native `Modal`, and `popup.success()` (CommonPopup — also a native Modal) opened **while the overlay was still visible**; the handler's `finally` then dismissed the overlay UNDERNEATH the popup. Dismissing the lower of two stacked native Modals on Android leaves touch routed to the dead window — same family as the §17 stranded-backdrop landmines. Fixed structurally: **the upload overlay is now a plain absolute `View`** (zIndex/elevation 9999) — still blocks taps behind it, cannot deadlock with any Modal — plus every upload handler now clears `uploadingLabel`/`imageUploading` BEFORE showing any popup (gallery add, replace, horoscope, profile photo, both success and error paths). ⚠️ **Rule: never show a popup while another native Modal is visible, and never dismiss a Modal that sits under one** — clear overlays first. tsc 56 unchanged. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-04 (night, batch 3) — Photos section: honest copy, 3 real gallery slots, X/3 count, replace-in-place
Client feedback on the profile page's Photos card, all four points shipped:
- **"High-quality boosts views by 3×" removed** — a stat we don't measure, implying a feature that doesn't exist. Now "Add up to 3 photos of yourself".
- **The count read `active/active` (1/1, 2/2, 3/3) and hid at zero.** Now always visible as **X/3** (0/3 from the start), progress bar over the fixed cap of 3.
- **Grid is now 3 GALLERY slots** — the amber profile-photo tile that occupied slot 1 (added 2026-08-13 to disambiguate it) is gone at the client's request; the grid previously showed only 2 of the 3 allowed gallery photos. Profile photo remains editable via the hero avatar and via "Set as profile photo" on any gallery image (which copies the URL — gallery image stays, verified in `setGalleryImageAsProfile`).
- **🔴 "Replace with a new photo" never replaced anything** — the sheet action just called `handleGalleryUpload()`, ADDING a new image (and at 3/3, dying on the limit error). Built properly cross-repo: new backend **`POST /gallery/replaceGalleryImage`** (multipart userId/galleryId/file, ownership-checked) uploads to R2 and **updates the SAME GalleryEntity row** — position in the grid preserved, and no delete-then-upload window where a failed upload loses the member's photo. App: `userApi.replaceGalleryImage(fd)` + `handleGalleryReplace(img)` wired to the sheet.

Also fixed a pre-existing `atob(string|null)` tsc error in the gallery upload — **baseline is now 56** (was 57). Needs backend deploy + APK rebuild.

### 2026-09-04 (night) — Live-launch bug batch 2: intermittent server stalls diagnosed, profile-page resilience, empty All Matches, delete-modal keyboard, friendly upload errors, decorative call chip
Client reports from the first live day, each verified before fixing:

**🔴 "Home sections empty / everything timing out" — the SERVER stalls intermittently, the app is fine.** Repro'd live: one probe returned Cloudflare **522 after 20s** while the same endpoints answered in 0.3–1.6s minutes later. During a stall EVERY endpoint times out at the app's 10s axios limit at once (home shelves + support unread count in the client's log). Root cause found in the backend: **`EmailService` used `HttpClient.newHttpClient()` — no connect or request timeout — and every email (OTP at signup, approval, rejection, plan-assigned) is sent SYNCHRONOUSLY in the request thread.** With open-in-view each hung request pins a Hikari connection (pool=20), so a few slow ZeptoMail calls freeze the whole API, then it recovers. Fixed: `connectTimeout(5s)` on the client + `.timeout(10s)` per request on BOTH senders. **Needs the backend deploy.** 💡 When "everything times out at once, then recovers," check thread/pool pinning before blaming any single endpoint.

**Profile page blank + edit popup not opening (new live member 915).** Two layers: (a) the fetch failing during a stall was SWALLOWED — `refreshProfile`'s catch only logged, rendering a fully blank profile with dead edit buttons; now shows an error Toast, and `handleEdit(undefined)` shows "pull down to refresh" instead of doing nothing. (b) **`formatUserDetails` did raw `JSON.parse` with no try/catch and assumed `familyInfo`/`astronomicInfo` are ARRAYS — new signups store `familyInfo` as an OBJECT** (verified on live user 915), and the new-signup key is `familyType` not `family_type`, so the Family section showed all "-" for every new member. Now per-field tolerant parse + array-or-object (`firstOf`) + both key spellings — matching what the render code at the bottom of the file already did.

**All Matches shelf now hides when empty** — wrapped `(isLoading || allMatches.length > 0)`, same pattern as the other three shelves (2026-08-30). ⚠️ Same file landmine: anchor edits on the unique shelf comment, not section names.

**Delete-account modal input hid behind the keyboard** — `KeyboardAvoidingView` had `behavior={ios ? 'padding' : undefined}`; with behavior undefined it does NOTHING on Android. Now `behavior="padding"` on both platforms — KAV computes the real overlap between its own frame and the keyboard, so it self-corrects whether or not the window resizes under edge-to-edge.

**Birth-chart/photo upload errors no longer show raw backend text** — `res.data.message`/`e.response.data.message` (Java exception strings) went straight into member popups. New `friendlyUploadError()` in profile.tsx: generic "Something went wrong. Please try again later.", raw message to console, **413 keeps its actionable "image too large" copy**. Applied to profile-photo/gallery/horoscope/set-as-profile branches + `components/tabs.tsx` photo add/delete + horoscope delete (its 3-photo-limit business message deliberately kept).

**Profile page call icon** — the phone chip next to the member's OWN number was a `TouchableOpacity` with no `onPress`; now a plain decorative `View`.

tsc 57 unchanged throughout. App changes ride the next APK; the email-timeout fix rides the backend deploy (same jar as resubmit + assign-plan).

Ionicons eye in a `pinRow` wrapper; the eye is absolutely positioned over the input's right edge with `paddingRight: 36` on the input so the 4 digits/placeholder never run under it; `secureTextEntry={!showPin}`. tsc 57 unchanged. Rides the next APK.

### 2026-09-04 — 🔴 Resubmit-after-rejection NEVER worked (endpoint didn't exist); note-to-admin added; signup label District
Client repro: admin rejects a profile, member taps Resubmit, "Something went wrong" — every time. **`POST /user/resubmit-profile/{encodedUserId}` did not exist in the backend at all** (zero grep hits; live server returns a raw Spring 404), so axios rejected and the catch showed the generic error. Same class as the 2026-08-30 Change PIN bug: the app calling something never built.

Built cross-repo:
- **Backend**: `UserService.resubmitProfile(encodedUserId, note)` + `POST /user/resubmit-profile/{encodedUserId}` (optional body `{message}` ≤250, capped server-side). APPROVED → 400; REJECTED/PENDING → sets PENDING and stores the note in the new `users.resubmission_note` column (250, ddl-auto creates it). The note is APPENDED as the last column of `findAllUsersForAdmin`'s select (never the middle — the mapper's `row.length > N` guards stay valid) and surfaced as `resubmissionNote` in `/admin/users` rows. `approveUser`/`rejectUser` both null it out — the note is consumed once acted on, otherwise a stale note reappears on the next rejection round.
- **App** (`ProfileUnderVerificationScreen.tsx`): "Message to our team (optional)" multiline `TextInput` under the rejection card — `maxLength={250}` + live counter, sent via `userApi.resubmitProfile(userId, message)`, cleared on success. Also fixed the screen's Contact Support dummies while here: WhatsApp `+91 98765 43210` → the real line `+91 79045 47565`, and `mailto:support@vaibhavvivaaha.com` (dead domain) → `selvakrish820@gmail.com`.
- **Admin panel** (⚠️ the LIVE repo is `VaibhavVivaahaAdmin`, not the stale `adminpanel` sibling — git history to 2026-08-08 + `deploy/` there; the mobile CLAUDE.md sister-doc pointer is outdated): verification/profiles rows render an amber "Member's note" strip (`col-span-full`) when `resubmissionNote` is present.

**Signup**: "City / District" label/placeholder/validation-name → "District" (data unchanged — same `districts` keyValue list, same `location` column).

Backend `./mvnw compile` clean; app tsc 57 unchanged; admin tsc clean in the touched file. **Needs backend deploy + admin deploy + APK rebuild** — until the backend deploy, resubmit keeps failing in production exactly as reported.

### 2026-08-31 — Support chat card ignored the robot's position, and its keyboard lift was an assumption
Client repro: tap the VVM Support robot, the card opens centred; with the keyboard up it should not be hidden. Two separate defects.

**The card never followed the robot.** `SupportFAB` is draggable, but the card's position was hardcoded (`bottom: insets.bottom + FOOTER_HEIGHT + FAB_SIZE + 12`, caret pinned `right: 30`) — so a robot dragged to the top-left still produced a bottom-right card with a caret pointing at nothing. A `cardAbove` variable existed for exactly this and was **dead code**: never referenced, and reading `translateY.value` (a Reanimated shared value) during render isn't reactive anyway. The FAB's Y is now mirrored into JS state on load and on every drag-end, the card anchors above the robot (or **below** it once it's in the top half), and the caret follows both the side (`fabOnRight`) and the edge, disappearing while the keyboard is up where it no longer touches the FAB. Horizontal centring was already correct (`width: SCREEN_W - 28` + `right: 14`) and is unchanged.

**🔴 The keyboard lift assumed `adjustResize` does NOT apply** — `bottom: keyboardHeight + 10`, unconditional. That is the same wrong assumption fixed twice before (chatscreen 2026-08-13, sign-up 2026-08-23): under edge-to-edge + targetSdk 36 whether Android honours it **varies by device**, and on one that does resize this lifts the card a second full keyboard height, off the top of the screen. Now measured the same way — the always-mounted `absoluteFillObject` FAB root reports its height via `onLayout`, the keyboard-closed height is the baseline, and if the container genuinely shrank (>100px, so a status-/nav-bar shift is never mistaken for a keyboard) the lift is **0**, otherwise the **full measured keyboard height**. A `[support-kb]` log mirrors `[chat-kb]`/`[signup-kb]`.

⚠️ **`maxHeight` was the second half of the hiding.** It was a fixed `SCREEN_H * 0.55`, but a keyboard takes ~40% of the screen, leaving ~60% — so even a correctly-lifted card ran under the status bar. It is now computed from the space actually left (floor 240, still capped at 55%).

💡 Same rule as the other two: **measure the container, never assume the resize.** tsc 57 unchanged. Needs an APK rebuild.

### 2026-08-30 — 🔴 Settings → Change PIN always failed: it read a key nothing ever writes
Client repro: changing the PIN from Settings shows "Phone number not found". It fails **100% of the time** — the feature has never worked.

`SettingPageChangePin.tsx` identified the member with `AsyncStorage.getItem('resetPhoneNumber')`. **That key has four readers and ZERO writers** — grep the whole app: `SettingPageChangePin`, `ChangePinScreen` and `OTPValidationScreen` (×2) all read it and nothing anywhere sets it. So `phoneNumber` was always null and the guard fired before any request went out. Confirmed against the live endpoint: `changePin` with `mobileNumber: null` returns *"User not found with this mobile number"*.

It is a leftover from a **mobile-OTP** reset flow that no longer exists. The live forgot-PIN path is **email-based** — `ResetPasswordScreen` writes `resetEmail`, `OTPValidationScreen` takes an `email` param and, on `purpose === 'reset'`, routes to `SetNewPasswordScreen` with a `resetToken`. That branch never touches `resetPhoneNumber` and never reaches `ChangePinScreen`.

Fixed: this screen is reached by a member who is **already signed in**, so the identity is simply their own number — `userData.mobileNumber` from `UserDataContext`, falling back to the `mobileNumber` AsyncStorage key that `LoginScreen` actually writes. The error copy also changed from "Phone number not found" (meaningless to a member) to "Could not identify your account. Please sign in again."

**Verified against production**, not just compiled: `changePin` with a real member number returns `200 "PIN updated successfully"` and the account still logs in afterwards.

⚠️ **The other two `resetPhoneNumber` readers are dead legacy paths** — `ChangePinScreen.tsx` and the non-email branch of `OTPValidationScreen`. They are unreachable while the reset flow stays email-based, so they were left alone; but if a mobile-OTP reset is ever reintroduced, that key needs a writer or those screens fail the same way. tsc 57 unchanged. Needs an APK rebuild.

### 2026-08-30 — 🔴 Tapping an empty home shelf CLOSED the app; empty shelves now hidden
Client repro: with no Daily Recommendation records, tapping that section killed the app. Same exposure on New Connections and Near You.

**Root cause — the endpoints return `code: 404` with `data: NULL`, not an empty array.** Verified live: `getDailyShuffledUsersByCaste`, `getTop30NewUsers` and `getUserDetailByCasteIdAndLocation` all answer that way when nothing matches (the usual HTTP-200-with-a-code-in-the-body pattern). `listProfile.tsx` assigned that null straight into state (`setProfileData(response.data.data)` — three cases had no `|| []`, unlike `interestMatches`/`allMatches` which did), so the render then evaluated `profileData.length` **on null**. A throw during render is not catchable by the screen's try/catch and takes the whole app down — hence "the app is closing" rather than an error toast.

Fixed in two places:
- **`listProfile.tsx`** — one `rows()` helper coerces every case to `[]` (`Array.isArray(res?.data?.data) ? … : []`), and both render guards are now `(profileData?.length ?? 0)`. Also deleted a debug `console.log("New Connections Data", response.data.data[0].userDetail)` that threw on an empty list *before* the data was ever set — which is why New Connections silently showed an empty list instead of crashing, masking half the bug.
- **`(tabs)/index.tsx`** — the three shelves now render only when `(isLoading || <list>.length > 0)`, matching the pattern the Interest-Based Matches shelf already used. Loading stays visible so the skeletons still show; a genuinely empty shelf disappears rather than presenting a header that leads nowhere.

⚠️ **The identical section comments (`{/* Daily Recommendations Section */}` etc.) appear TWICE in this file** — once in the loading-skeleton component near the top and once in the real render past line 700. A first pass keyed on the comment text corrupted the skeleton component; the edit must be anchored past line 700. tsc 57 unchanged. Needs an APK rebuild.

### 2026-08-23 — 🔴 Signup PIN / Confirm PIN stayed under the Android keyboard: the scroll had nowhere to go
`enableOnAndroid` and `enableAutomaticScroll` were already `true` (fixed 2026-07-15), so the usual suspects were not the cause. The real reason: **PIN and Confirm PIN are the LAST elements inside page 2's `KeyboardAwareScrollView`, the footer CTA sits OUTSIDE it, and `contentContainerStyle` had `paddingBottom: 0`.** A ScrollView cannot scroll past its own content end — with nothing below the last field there was no scroll range left, so no amount of auto-scroll could lift it above the keyboard.

Fixed by giving the scroll somewhere to go — but **how much is MEASURED, not assumed**, using the same technique as `chatscreen.tsx` (2026-08-13), which is the only approach that proved reliable on device.

Three attempts preceded it and all were per-device guesses: a fixed `paddingBottom` (tuned 260 → 200 → 170 → 130), then the full measured keyboard height (over-scrolled — the form could be dragged well past its last field), then 60%-of-keyboard-minus-25 (a fudge factor with no principle behind it). The problem is that `windowSoftInputMode="adjustResize"` is set but, **under edge-to-edge / targetSdk 36, whether Android honours it varies by device** — so assuming it applied leaves the field behind the keyboard, and assuming it did not double-compensates.

Now: `SafeAreaView` reports its height via `onLayout`; the keyboard-closed height is kept as a baseline; if the container **genuinely shrank** (>100px, so a status-/nav-bar shift is never mistaken for a keyboard) the OS already made room and the spacer is **0**, otherwise it is the **full measured keyboard height**. Self-correcting on either kind of device. Android `extraScrollHeight` 20 → 45. A `[signup-kb]` log mirrors chatscreen's `[chat-kb]` — `console` is not stripped in release here, so `adb logcat | grep signup-kb` settles what a specific device actually did.

⚠️ **Safe from feedback** because the padding is applied to the scroll view's CONTENT, which never changes the measured container's own frame. Applying it to the container itself would oscillate.

💡 **Diagnostic rule: when a field will not clear the keyboard, check whether there is scrollable content BELOW it before touching the keyboard props.** A focused field that is last in a container with no bottom padding is unliftable by design.

⚠️ Page 1 is now less exposed anyway — after the same-day reorder its last element is the Subcaste *picker*, which opens no keyboard; every keyboard field sits above the two dropdowns. tsc 57 unchanged. Needs an APK rebuild.

### 2026-08-23 — LoginScreen felt loose: draggable with the keyboard closed, and stayed displaced after
Two separate causes, both in the `KeyboardAwareScrollView` props:
- **No bounce guard.** The login form fits the screen with the keyboard closed, but a ScrollView still lets you drag and rubber-band it. Added `bounces={false}`, `alwaysBounceVertical={false}`, `overScrollMode="never"` — movement stops when the content fits, while a genuine overflow (small screen, large system font) can still scroll, so nothing gets trapped off-screen. **Deliberately NOT `scrollEnabled={false}`**, which would trap the CTA on a short device.
- **`enableResetScrollToCoords={false}`** meant the form stayed wherever the keyboard had pushed it after dismissal — the other half of the "unstable" feel. Now `true` with `resetScrollToCoords={{x:0,y:0}}`. It fires on keyboard hide, not on focus change, so moving between the mobile and PIN fields does not reset mid-form.

⚠️ `sign-up.tsx` uses the same component but genuinely overflows (long form), so it was left alone — a bounce guard there would be harmless but the reset would fight the multi-field flow. tsc 57 unchanged. Needs an APK rebuild.

### 2026-08-23 (later) — Districts alphabetised; signup Age+Email row moved above the fold
**`districts` reordered alphabetically** (Ariyalur → Virudhunagar), with **`Other` deliberately excluded from the sort and pinned last** — alphabetising it into the middle buries the escape hatch among real districts. Ids renumbered 1–39; safe because `users.location` stores the **string**, not the id, so nothing already saved changes meaning. Verified live: 39 entries, sorted, `Other` last.

**`sign-up.tsx` page 1: the Age + Email row moved to sit directly under DOB + Gender**, ahead of Caste/Subcaste/Mobile. Client report: on Android, tapping Email opened the keyboard *over the field*. It previously sat near the bottom of the page, so there was a lot of form above it and `KeyboardAwareScrollView` had further to travel; higher up there is much less to scroll past. Age is auto-derived from DOB, so it now also sits beside the field that produces it. **Mobile Number was then moved up too**, to sit directly after Age+Email and before Caste — same reason (it is a keyboard field, and Caste/Subcaste are pickers that do not raise one). Final page-1 order: First/Last Name → DOB+Gender → Age+Email → Mobile → Caste → Subcaste, i.e. **every keyboard-entry field is now above the two dropdowns**, so nothing typed sits near the bottom of the scroll where the keyboard covers it. Pure JSX move — no logic, validation or state touched (`validateFormData()` runs once on final submit, not per field position). Verified: tsc 57 unchanged, HStack tags balanced 4/4 across the page-1 region. **Needs an APK rebuild** — the districts change does not (server-side keyValue).

### 2026-08-23 — `districts` keyValue trimmed to Tamil Nadu; 🔴 two spellings made 109 profiles unsearchable
**The dropdown/data mismatch disease again** — same shape as star, dosham, education and income. Location search is `LOWER(u.location) LIKE %value%`, and two dropdown entries matched almost nothing:
- **`Tiruchirappalli` matched 0 profiles** — 59 were stored as `Trichy`.
- **`Tiruppur` matched 1** — 50 were stored as `Tirupur` (one `p`; `%tiruppur%` cannot match `Tirupur`).

Fixed by normalising the DATA, not the list (the dropdown should carry official district names): backup `_bak_users_location_20260823` (758 rows), then `Trichy → Tiruchirappalli` and `Tirupur → Tiruppur`. **110 profiles became searchable.**

**List rewritten to Tamil Nadu only** — all **38 official districts**, keeping the client's existing ids 1–36 so nothing already stored shifts meaning, plus the two that were missing from their list (**Kanyakumari**, **Tiruvarur**), then `Other` last. Removed: Bangalore, Hyderabad, Mumbai, Delhi, Kolkata, Pune, Kochi, Pondicherry. Backup of the previous row in `/tmp/districts_backup_20260823.txt`.

⚠️ **6 existing profiles still hold a removed location** (Pune ×5, Kolkata ×1). Their stored string is untouched and they remain visible; they simply cannot be found via the district dropdown any more, and re-editing their profile will require picking a TN district or `Other`. Left as-is deliberately — rewriting a member's stated location is worse than the dropdown gap.

Verified: live API returns 39 entries, no non-TN values; `verify-search.py` 19/19.

💡 **This is the fifth field with this bug.** When adding ANY new dropdown backed by free-text data, check the stored values match the list — `SELECT DISTINCT <col>` against the keyValue list before shipping.

### 2026-08-20 — targetSdk 35 → 36 (Android 16), required by Play from 2026-08-31
Play flagged the app as non-compliant: from **31 Aug 2026** an app whose target API is not within a year of the latest Android release can no longer be updated. Bumped in **`android/gradle.properties` lines 67–68** (`android.compileSdkVersion` / `android.targetSdkVersion` — NOT in `app/build.gradle`, which reads them via `rootProject.ext`).

**This was a two-line config change, not an upgrade project** — the toolchain was already capable: android-36 platform + build-tools 36.1.0 installed, and **AGP 8.11.0** (API 36 needs ≥8.9).

The four Android 16 behaviour changes were each checked against this app before bumping:
- **Edge-to-edge is enforced on 36 (no opt-out).** Already `edgeToEdgeEnabled=true`, and the bugs it caused were fixed on 2026-08-12 (chat keyboard measurement, `StarMatchResult`'s react-native `SafeAreaView`, home status-bar inset). Lowest-risk path precisely because that work was already done.
- **Predictive back.** The manifest sets `android:enableOnBackInvokedCallback="false"`, so the app is opted out and legacy `BackHandler` still works — load-bearing, since LoginScreen's `BackHandler.exitApp()` fix depends on it. **If predictive back is ever enabled, re-test that fix.**
- **Orientation lock ignored on large screens.** `orientation: portrait` still holds on phones; tablets may rotate. Cosmetic.
- **16 KB page alignment.** `expo.useLegacyPackaging=false` is the 16KB-friendly setting; RN 0.81 + AGP 8.11 handle it.

**Verified in the built bundle**: merged manifest declares `targetSdkVersion="36"` / `minSdkVersion="24"`; signature still matches the upload key; all four removed permissions still absent; `DETECT_SCREEN_CAPTURE` still present; 3 onboarding assets present. `versionCode` is 2. Build ~14 min.

⚠️ **Still needs on-device testing of the three edge-to-edge-sensitive screens** (chat with keyboard open, Home/StarMatchResult insets, LoginScreen hardware back) — API 36 enforces edge-to-edge more strictly than 35.

### 2026-08-19 — Play release build: real upload key, Razorpay dropped, screenshot flag caught
**🔴 `release` was signed with `signingConfigs.debug`** — the untouched RN template default. That produces an AAB Play always rejects ("signed in debug mode"). No release keystore had ever been generated. Created `android/app/vvm-upload.jks` (RSA 2048, alias `vvm-upload`, valid to 2054); credentials in **`android/keystore.properties`** (chmod 600, git-ignored). ⚠️ **NOT in `android/gradle.properties` — that file is tracked by git**, so anything put there gets committed. `build.gradle` now throws a `GradleException` naming the missing file rather than silently debug-signing, so this cannot regress. Verified: the built AAB's SHA-256 matches the upload key exactly.

**🔴 `.env` had `EXPO_PUBLIC_ALLOW_SCREENSHOTS=true`** left over from screenshot capture — shipping that disables `FLAG_SECURE` for every member. Commented out with a warning; confirmed absent from the built bundle. **Check this before every release build.**

**Removed `react-native-razorpay`** (+ its `@types`, and its two `expo.install.exclude` / doctor allow-list entries). It was **never imported anywhere** — zero references in any source file, not autolinked — but it pulled **`android.permission.READ_PHONE_STATE`** into the merged manifest via `com.razorpay:core`. That is a sensitive permission Play makes you justify, for a dead dependency, and it contradicted the privacy policy's "no third-party payment gateway" statement. No code needed commenting out. ⚠️ `npm install` fails with ERESOLVE on a **pre-existing** eslint peer conflict — use `--legacy-peer-deps` (this project was installed that way; there is no `.npmrc`). Stale `ios/Pods/razorpay-*` artifacts remain and clear on the next `pod install`.

⚠️ **`./gradlew clean bundleRelease` FAILS** with `CMake ... react_codegen_rnasyncstorage is not built by this project` — `clean` wipes codegen artifacts CMake still references. Use `rm -rf app/.cxx app/build/generated/autolinking app/build/intermediates/cxx && ./gradlew bundleRelease` instead. Full build ~10 min.

**`READ_MEDIA_IMAGES` removed via manifest override** (`tools:node="remove"` in the app manifest). It is declared by **`expo-screen-capture`, not the image picker** — verified by reading the library manifests directly, and it is scoped `minSdkVersion=33 maxSdkVersion=33`, i.e. **Android 13 only**. Its sole purpose is letting `addScreenshotListener` read the screenshot from MediaStore — but the app calls `preventScreenCaptureAsync()`, which sets `FLAG_SECURE` and stops the screenshot from ever being taken, so on Android that listener can never fire. It does real work only on iOS, where blocking is impossible. Removing it costs nothing on Android and **eliminates Play's Photo & Video Permissions declaration entirely**. Photo SELECTION is unaffected: `expo-image-picker` uses the Android Photo Picker (`photopicker_activity` metadata) and declares no media permission. ⚠️ **Never justify this permission to Google — remove it and re-upload instead;** justifying a permission you don't need invites scrutiny of a use case that doesn't exist. ⚠️ Do NOT trace permission sources with `grep -A2` on the manifest-merger report — the context window spills into the next permission's block and misattributes it; read `node_modules/<pkg>/android/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml` directly.

**Confirmed absent from the bundle**: `RECORD_AUDIO`, `SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW` (removed 2026-08-18), `READ_PHONE_STATE` (Razorpay), `READ_MEDIA_IMAGES`. Present and expected: `CAMERA`/`READ_MEDIA_IMAGES` (expo-image-picker), `DETECT_SCREEN_CAPTURE` (expo-screen-capture), `USE_BIOMETRIC`/`USE_FINGERPRINT` (androidx.biometric — harmless, no justification needed).

### 2026-08-18 — 🔴 Back button on LoginScreen walked a logged-OUT user into the profile page
Client repro: log out → LoginScreen shows a back chevron → tapping it lands on the profile page of the account that just signed out.

**`router.canGoBack()` is NOT a sufficient guard.** The earlier fix gated the chevron on it, but logout crosses a navigator group boundary — `(tabs)` → `(main)` — and the authenticated stack stays in history, so `canGoBack()` keeps returning true and `router.back()` re-enters it. The screen still holds live state, so it renders the signed-out member's data.

Fixed by removing the back button from LoginScreen entirely: **every** route to it is a `router.replace()` — onboarding, logout, delete-account, sign-up, PIN reset, `SetNewPassword` — so a legitimate back target never exists. (LoginScreen → sign-up is a `push`, so *sign-up* correctly keeps its own back.)

⚠️ **The chevron was only half the bug** — Android's hardware back reached the same stale stack. A `useFocusEffect` + `BackHandler` on LoginScreen now calls `BackHandler.exitApp()` instead: login is the app's root, so leaving the app is the expected behaviour, and merely swallowing the event (`return true`) would make back feel broken.

**If another entry point to LoginScreen is ever added, it must use `replace`, not `push`** — a pushed login would have a real back target and this screen no longer renders one.

### 2026-08-18 — Screenshot escape hatch (`EXPO_PUBLIC_ALLOW_SCREENSHOTS`) + demo-account sample data
**Why you cannot screenshot this app:** `app/_layout.tsx` calls `ScreenCapture.preventScreenCaptureAsync()`, which sets **`FLAG_SECURE`** on Android. That blocks *every* capture path — hardware buttons, `adb shell screencap`, `scrcpy`, and screen recording all return blank — so Play Store listing images cannot be produced from a normal build. The guard is a deliberate member-privacy feature and was NOT removed.

Instead the guard is now skipped when `process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_ALLOW_SCREENSHOTS === 'true'`. **Defaults to OFF**, so any normal build is protected exactly as before. Build once with the flag, capture, then discard that build — it logs a console warning naming itself unshippable. ⚠️ **Never ship a build with that env var set**; it disables screenshot protection for every member.

**Demo account (706) seeded for capture** — it had 1 conversation and 1 message, so Chat List and Chat would have photographed as empty screens. Added 4 conversations / 15 messages (natural matrimony threads, staggered timestamps so the list sorts realistically, `isRead` set), the matching `APPROVED` interestRequest rows (chat is gated on mutual acceptance), and 4 shortlists so the profile stats are non-zero. All tagged `createdBy='seed-demo-2026-08-18'` — one DELETE removes them. Verified live: `conversation/chatlist/NzA2` returns 5 threads with correct last-message previews.

🔴 **Do NOT put the current profile photos in a store listing.** Of 92 visible profiles with photos, **80 are hotlinked from Pinterest (53), Wix (19) and Reddit (8)** — photographs of real people, used without licence or consent; only 12 are on our own R2. Publishing those as our members is a copyright and personality-rights exposure and misrepresentation under Play's Store Listing policy. Replace the ~8–10 profiles that appear in the screenshots with owned/AI-generated portraits before capturing.

### 2026-08-18 — First-run onboarding flow: splash → 3 intro screens → login
New flow requested by the client (previously: splash → a single landing screen with Get Started/Sign In). Now: **native splash → `(main)/onboarding.tsx` (first run only) → LoginScreen**; logout and every later cold start land on LoginScreen directly.

**New `app/(root)/(main)/onboarding.tsx`** — 3 slides. **Layout note (client-reported fix, same day):** the first cut rendered the artwork full-bleed with the controls absolutely positioned on top, and on taller screens the Next/Get Started bar sat over the images' baked-in subtitles. Now the slides are padded (rounded 22px, maroon `#400102` page bg), the image uses `resizeMode="contain"` so its text can never be cropped or covered, and the dots/buttons live in a **reserved band below the Swiper in normal flex flow** — a sibling, not an overlay, so overlap is structurally impossible. 3 slides (`react-native-swiper`, same lib as sign-up), client-supplied artwork bundled as `assets/images/onboard{1,2,3}.webp` (1080×1935, ~180 KB each). ⚠️ **The source designs had pagination dots and a "Get Started" button PAINTED INTO the artwork** — those were erased from the bundled images with Pillow (background-sampled patches) because painted controls can't be tapped; the screen renders real dots/Skip/Next/Get Started styled to match. If the artwork is ever regenerated, re-do that erasure — the originals are in `~/Downloads/Create_a_premium_full-screen_mobile_2026081815{47,53,54}.jpeg`, and note the files' timestamps do NOT match their screen order (1554 is screen 2, 1553 is screen 3 — verified by content, my first pass got this wrong and cut a subtitle in half).

**Artwork replaced same day (final set):** the client supplied a fresh 3-image set — 1) "Families First. Compatibility Matters." 2) "A Match Worth Trusting" 3) "Where Two Hearts Unite, Two Families Become One." — none of which have baked-in dots/buttons, so **no erasure step this time** (the erasure note below applies only if artwork with painted controls ever comes back). The three files have three different aspect ratios AND three different edge maroons, so `SLIDES` carries a per-slide sampled `bg` (#260106 / #2F0000 / #350203) and the images render `contain` — matched letterboxing reads as full-bleed. Sources: `~/Downloads/ChatGPT Image Aug 18, 2026, 04_{21_08,24_26,28_53} PM.png` (the 04_25_19 file is a rejected wider variant of screen 3 — identify these by CONTENT, not timestamp). Dots/CTA sized down on request (7px rings, 18px active pill, 184×44 gradient button). **All three files were then normalised to one ratio (1080×1919, 9:16, matching screen 3)** at the client's request: screens 1–2 were taller (0.462/0.474), so they were EXTENDED sideways onto the 9:16 canvas — nothing cropped — with mirrored+blurred+darkened wings continuing each image's own edges (a flat edge-colour smear was tried first and showed visible streak bands in the bright mid-sections). **Final order (client, same day): 1) Two Hearts Unite 2) A Match Worth Trusting (bordered variant) 3) Families First** — all rebuilt from the ORIGINAL PNGs, never re-processed from the shipped webps. ⚠️ **Slides use `resizeMode="contain"`, not `cover`, and the 3% zoom was removed** — every artwork in this set has a decorative gold frame only **~2.2% inside its edge** (measured), and `cover` crops a fixed FRACTION of the width (~2–5%/side on a tall phone) which clips it. Widening the images cannot fix this: the crop scales proportionally, so any fixed-percentage inset is eaten regardless. `contain` + each slide's own sampled edge colour as its letterbox still reads as full-bleed. **If artwork without edge decoration is ever supplied, `cover` becomes safe again.** ✅ **Final state: all three sources are natively 9:16, so NO margin synthesis is used anywhere** — each webp is a straight resize of its source PNG. Getting here took three tries on slide 3 and the lesson is worth keeping: a 0.462-ratio source was first extended with mirrored/blurred copies of its own scene (dark muddy side bands — looked like a different app), then with slide 1's sampled maroon (better, but still fabricated). Both were discarded once a **natively 9:16 version of the same artwork** turned up. **Never fabricate margins — ask for the artwork at 1080×1919 instead.** Sources: `~/Downloads/ChatGPT Image Aug 18, 2026, 04_{28_53, 45_42, 18_05} PM.png` for slides 1/2/3 respectively; identify by CONTENT, several near-identical variants of each design exist and the filenames/timestamps do not follow slide order.

**`onboardingSeen` (new AsyncStorage key) must SURVIVE logout** — that is the whole "after logout show login only, never the intro again" rule. New **`utils/storage.ts` → `clearStorageForLogout()`** (getAllKeys + multiRemove of everything except a `SURVIVES_LOGOUT` allowlist) replaces all three raw `AsyncStorage.clear()` sites (AuthContext stale-session wipe, AuthContext `handleLogout`, settingsPage delete-account). ⚠️ **Never reintroduce a raw `AsyncStorage.clear()` in a logout path** — it replays the intro. This change also fixes the section-17 landmine where `handleLogout`'s clear() was fire-and-forget: it is now awaited, so a fast logout→relogin can no longer have the delayed wipe eat the new session's writes.

**Landing screen retired** — `(main)/index.tsx` is now a pure router hub (kept because settingsPage logout + delete-account still route to `'/(root)/(main)'` bare): restores a valid stored session to tabs as before, otherwise redirects to LoginScreen. Its `wedding.webp` (197 KB) was orphaned and deleted from assets. `app/index.tsx` gained the `onboardingSeen` branch. **LoginScreen's back chevron is now wrapped in `router.canGoBack()`** — login is the flow's root on cold start, so the old unconditional back button pointed at nothing (it still shows when login is pushed from sign-up). The existing "Sign Up" link (line ~513) is the register path, since the landing's Get Started button is gone.

Deliberately NOT reused: the existing `hasStarted` key — it is written by LoginScreen and removed by ProfileUnderVerificationScreen, i.e. it means "has logged in", a different lifecycle than "has seen the intro".

**Verification:** `npx tsc --noEmit` 57, unchanged baseline. Needs the APK rebuild (bundled images + flow are client-side only; no backend change).

### 2026-08-18 — Last 58 `Vann*/Naidu*/Aadi*/Mudal*/Free* TestMale` accounts renamed
The caste × plan test matrix (696–755) still carried names like `VannSilver TestFemale` and `MudalPlatinum TestMale`. They are all `isActive='N'` so they never reach member search, but they are visible in the admin panel and would be the moment anyone reactivates one. All 58 now have real Tamil names, generated against the full existing-name set so **no name collides with the 322 already in the database**.

⚠️ **The old names encoded caste + plan + gender at a glance, and that is now lost.** The mapping (userId · old name · new name · plan) is saved at **`VaibhavVivaahaBackend/test-accounts-map.txt`** — keep it, or tier-specific testing means looking up the plan for every account. Mobiles are unchanged (`91xxxxxxxx` pattern still encodes caste+tier: `9114000002` = Vanniyar Silver female), so the number remains the quickest way to identify one.
